# 23. Full audit, 2026-11-13: bugs, gaps, every sound, every loop kit

Written by the main research session from a 27-agent audit and research workflow (5 audit lanes, 7 research lanes, 12 adversarial verifiers, 3 design leads). Build audited: e94ae83. Nothing in the repo was changed by the audit. Refuted claims are dropped from the design docs (25, 26); the fact-check lists below keep them visible.

User (verbatim): "we need to audit this whole plug in, figure out whats not working whats bugged what sounds need more mysterious texture a world a mystic a feeling, make them feel more like the music that i listen to and like rare sounds ... audit the whole plug in look for whats wrong and come up with ideas to make it look and feel more authentic and unique and rare sounds".

Companion docs: 24 (research with sources), 25 (sound plan: texture upgrades, 36 new instruments, 24 new kits, 14 generators, 14 texture stages), 26 (authentic look and feel).

## 1. Bugs (merged, deduplicated, verified)

### B1. LOOPS latch goes stale after a kit change or a tempo feel flip: keys stay lit and the next press is swallowed

- Severity: major; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by code path and HISE 4.1.0 source. Not run in Live. loadKit (Interface.js:3768-3781) sets S.loopQ = -1 and calls applyFeel. So LoopSampler.setTimestretchOptions (:627) runs on every kit change, and loadSampleMap runs too whenever the map differs. Both go through killAllVoicesAndCall, and KillStateHandler then kills the whole main synth chain (KillStateHandler.cpp:81, https://github.com/christophhart/HISE/blob/4.1.0/hi_core/hi_core/KillStateHandler.cpp; ModulatorSampler.cpp:1035-1063, https://github.com/christophhart/HISE/blob/4.1.0/hi_core/hi_sampler/sampler/ModulatorSampler.cpp). Only setLatch (:3847-3855) and setMode (:3882-3884) bump Globals.latchEpoch or clear latchMask. A triage grep confirms loadKit touches neither, and it never calls buildVD. KeyShift.js:72-81 keeps LATCH[n] pointing at a voice that no longer exists. noteOffDelayedByEventId does not throw on a dead id (ScriptingApi.cpp:5352-5397), so the next press reaches ignoreEvent(true) and is eaten as a stop. syncLatchKeys (:4018) keeps the stale tint lit. The same happens when the host tempo crosses the 0.7 / 1.45 feel thresholds (onTempo at :3905, and the check about once a second at :4017). Scenario: latch K1 and K2, then click another kit. The loops go silent but C3 and C#3 stay lit, and every lane that was latched needs a double press.
- Fix: In loadKit, and in applyFeel only when it actually calls setTimestretchOptions: bump Globals.latchEpoch, set Globals.latchMask = 0, and call clearLoopHeld(). A later feature that re-triggers latched lanes after a kit swap must go through KeyShift, the non-deferred script that owns LATCH. Otherwise the re-triggered voices are untracked.

### B2. In LOOPS the macro, ATTACK and RELEASE knobs show values that are not what you hear, and moving them or pressing VARIATION undoes the clean loop FX and rewrites the instrument preset

- Severity: major; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source. This merges the UX lane finding with the verifier's VARIATION addition. setPage shows MacroCard and A/R whenever the mode is not SAMPLES (Interface.js:2891-2904). applyLoopFx (:3785-3797) sets TONE 20 kHz, SPACE 0.08 and no ECHO/GRIT/PUMP, but leaves the knobs where they were. onMacro (:2495-2500) has no mode gate. Example: SPACE reads 60% while the loops play 8% wet, and one nudge jumps the reverb to 0.6 x 0.55 = 0.33 wet (:466). A/R drive only Amp Env and Sub Env (:479-489), and both synths are bypassed in LOOPS. Loop Lines has its own Loop Env (XML L388) that nothing drives. VARIATION (:2629, variation() at :2604-2616) is not mode-gated either. In LOOPS it randomises all 8 macros into the shared master FX, which brings TONE cutoff, ECHO, GRIT and PUMP back onto the loops, and it changes host parameters. RESET in LOOPS reapplies the clean FX without moving the knobs (:2587-2591). The MACROS strip still prints the instrument's ECHO/PUMP line (:2572).
- Fix: Gate onMacro, VARIATION and RESET by S.mode. In LOOPS, either show a LOOP FX card with its own non-host state that also drives Loop Env attack and release, or hide the macros and A/R. At minimum, set the knobs to the applyLoopFx values on entering LOOPS and restore the instrument values on leaving.

### B3. Tine EP renders start every note with a DC step, tine chord onsets inside loops step up to 0.85 FS, and about 30 loop takes click at the seam

- Severity: major; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by measurement on the installed renders (scripts in /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-noxvitae/0e6ab598-c428-4549-a613-b529408ad567/scratchpad/wf_mystic/verify_code/). Triage re-read sigbank_dsp.py:309: `x = x - np.mean(x[:2048])` runs after the 1.5 ms amp envelope, so sample 0 steps to -mean, and the 30 Hz hp at :310 passes the step. Corrected counts from a recount of all 2,752 SIG files: 83 of 302 tine_ep files have |x0| above 5 percent of peak. Every Perreo Keys file starts near -0.15 FS (SIG_perreo_keys_36: -0.151 FS, 23.7 percent of peak). Supersaw (132 of 829), prepared (25 of 44), 808 (12 of 94) and organ (2 of 42) also start nonzero, but the amp attack masks it, so only tine is a real DC transient. Loops: render_loop has no per-note attack ramp, and LoopXFade is 0 (build_loop_lines.py:188, :240). LL_neon_perreo_keys1 starts at -0.845 FS and ends at 0.000, and its beat-4 chord onset steps 0.847. keys2 starts at -0.498, keys3 at -0.269, keys4 at -0.251. A curvature click detector against local content finds about 30 real seam discontinuities (keys 19, bass 8, air 3). The code lane's '33 of 44' and '84 takes: supersaw 19, 808 15' were refuted; the second was a raw-step count inflated by bright content. The mean subtraction dates from b55d62b, not ba64ded.
- Fix: Primary fix at sigbank_dsp.py:309: drop the constant subtraction (the 30 Hz hp already removes DC), or multiply it by the attack envelope. Then re-render the tine presets and every kit that uses them, and re-run the ba64ded pitch check. In render_loop, fade only the loop tail toward x[0], or fade only sustained lanes, so drum and pluck beat-1 transients stay sharp. A seam ramp alone leaves the mid-loop onset clicks. CI: the proposed |x0| < 0.01 FS gate would fail today on 310 of 2,752 SIG files and 460 of 1,024 LL takes. Land it only together with a render-wide 1-2 ms fade-in and a curvature-based seam metric.

### B4. SAMPLES FORM pills (LOOP / ONE-SHOT / ALL) never change the filter, and the g / ge / sp / f pills never highlight on hover

- Severity: major; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source; triage re-read SamplesPage.js:884 and :1057. :884 returns "f" + Math.floor((x - 12) / 77.4). At 4.1.0, Math.floor returns std::floor((double)value), a double var (https://github.com/christophhart/HISE/blob/4.1.0/hi_scripting/scripting/engine/JavascriptEngineMathObject.cpp). Integral doubles stringify as "1.0"; this is the same root cause as the f41a786 kit pager that read "1.0-7.0/64". :1057 requires h.length == 2, so clicks on LOOP, ONE-SHOT and ALL all do nothing. SM.form only changes when user.json is restored. The same cause hits the hover ids "g5.0", "ge4.0", "sp1.0" and "f1.0" (:872, :893, :1271, :884): they never equal the paint-side ids (:962, :989, :997, :1338), so those pills never highlight. The g, ge and sp clicks still work because their handlers use parseInt.
- Fix: Build the ids from ints: "f" + Math.round(Math.floor(...)). Math.round returns an int at 4.1.0 (MathObject.cpp:159-162). Do the same for g, ge and sp. Add a selfTest: smpRailHit(50, 160) == "f0". Fix this together with the decimal-counter bug, which has the same root cause.

### B5. RESCAN swaps the PLAY KEYS, SIMILAR, selected and playing sounds because array positions survive the index reload

- Severity: major; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source. smpLoadIndex (SamplesPage.js:209-281) replaces SM.items (:228) and SM.idx but keeps SM.playKeys, SM.like, SM.playIt, SM.want and SM.sel, which are positions in the old array. The indexer sorts by source, then path (samples_index.py:263-273), so one new download shifts every later position. smpKeysChanged (:705), the clock (:1463) and smpEnter then call smpSetPlayKeys(SM.playKeys) against the new array and write the neighbouring sound's id to PlayItem (:680), which the Live set saves. If the index shrinks, SM.items[pos] is undefined and smpLoopNq / base.sim throw (:425). Two lower-impact races: (a) a heart or play still inside the 1 s smpSaveSoon window is overwritten when smpLoadUser runs (:244); (b) a refilter inside the 80 ms start wait plays slot r of the old map while the UI marks a different item (:603-611).
- Fix: Store ids, not positions (playKeysId, likeId, selId, playingId). Map them back through SM.idx after the reload and drop any that are gone. Stop the audition and clear SM.want before replacing SM.items, and clear SM.want in smpRefilter as well. Call smpSaveUser before smpLoadUser, or merge the two.

### B6. One-shot auditions pitched down for key match are released at the file length, so 808 tails are cut

- Severity: major; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source and XML; triage re-read :1443: `SM.offAt = SM.startAt + SM.items[SM.playIt].dur + 0.05`. The zone Root is slot - smpShift(it) (:451). Sample Hits has PitchTracking 1, OneShot 0 and a 50 ms envelope release (XML around L456). The total shift can be large: smpShift gives -6..+5, SEMI adds -6..+6 and OCT adds steps of 12 (:96-106). Example: a 2.0 s F 808 shifted -5 st rings for 2.67 s but is released at 2.05 s. With OCT -1, every one-shot audition stops at half its real length. The hit playhead (:1324) also uses it.dur.
- Fix: Set offAt = startAt + it.dur * Math.pow(2, -smpShift(it) / 12) + release, and use the same factor for the playhead. Do not switch Sample Hits to OneShot: smpStopAudition, Esc and up/down stepping rely on note-offs, and long 808 tails would stack across auditions.

### B7. Preset load and VARIATION destroy the user's SHAPE, MOD and macro edits with no undo, and the v0.1 A/B compare is gone

- Severity: major; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source. loadPreset (Interface.js:3693-3719) runs setDeepFromPreset and applyPresetMod. applyPresetMod resets every LFO and envelope source and calls Matrix.clearAllConnections (:3651-3672, :3660). The header prev/next arrows (:1043-1044) and every browser row click call loadPreset. variation() (:2604-2616) moves TONE by up to about +/-0.6 octave and the other macros by +/-0.12, with no snapshot. CHANGELOG.md L14 (0.1.0) lists an 'A/B macro compare' that no longer exists in code, so this is a regression. UserPresetWidgets.js is never included (includes at :23-27 and :3747). AppData 'User Presets/Factory' holds 5 v0.1 folders that no page browses.
- Fix: Take a one-step UNDO snapshot before every loadPreset and VARIATION: 8 macros, A/R, every DV and MV value, and Matrix.toBase64(). Restore A and B slots on the MACROS tape. Delete UserPresetWidgets.js or wire it up. Saving the user's own sounds is listed under gaps.

### B8. A hidden, always-on Air EQ colours the SAMPLES audition and the 'clean' LOOPS FX (+2.5 dB at 120 Hz, +2 dB at 9 kHz)

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source; both lanes reported this. XML L66 has Air EQ with Bypassed=0. Band0-4: +2.5 dB, 120 Hz, Q 0.7, enabled, type 2 (LowShelf). Band5-9: +2.0 dB, 9 kHz, Q 0.7, enabled, type 3 (HighShelf). Band order and the type enum come from CurveEq.h:68-86 at 4.1.0 (https://github.com/christophhart/HISE/blob/4.1.0/hi_core/hi_modules/effects/fx/CurveEq.h). smpRawFx (SamplesPage.js:1505-1513) and applyLoopFx (Interface.js:3785-3797) bypass the other FX but not this one. A triage grep finds no script that references "Air EQ". SAMPLES runs smpRawFx(true) on entry (:1520). As a result, an 808 or bass auditioned in the plug-in sounds fuller and brighter than the same file dragged into Live.
- Fix: Add FX_Air = Synth.getEffect("Air EQ") and call setBypassed(raw) in both smpRawFx and applyLoopFx. Then expose AIR on an FX page or fold it into TONE, so it is no longer a hidden fixed colour.

### B9. Mac keys always play velocity 127, and an octave shift made in PLAY carries into LOOPS, where most Mac keys then go silent

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source. KeyShift.js:55-58 identifies Mac keys as channel 16, notes 60-76, velocity 127. Every SIG map splits velocity at 85, so laptop-key melodies always hit the top layer. setMode (Interface.js:3858-3887) never resets S.shift or Globals.keyShift. The loop zones are 60-75, so after one ']' in PLAY only 72-75 (A, W, S, E: the AIR lane) play in LOOPS. The piano labels follow the shift (macKeyFor, :2664-2668), but the LOOPS help text 'Mac keys A to P' (:1139) does not change. Inferred edge case: a mouse click on 60-76 that happens to land at velocity 127 is treated as a Mac key and shifted.
- Fix: In KeyShift, right after a note is identified as a Mac key, call Message.setVelocity(Globals.macVel). Set that value with digit keys 1-5 (about 40/64/90/110/127) through Content.setKeyPressCallback, the way '[' and ']' work, and show it in the header. A separate Mac-key channel is not an option: clicks and QWERTY both arrive on channel 16 (inferred). On entering LOOPS, temporarily zero the shift, or print the shifted letters.

### B10. MOD pitch depth snaps to whole semitones, so tape wow, drift and subtle vibrato are impossible from the matrix

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified; triage re-read Interface.js:3583: `local val = c.Target == "A Pitch" ? Math.round(frac * 24.0 - 12.0) : frac;`. New pitch routes start at 1.0 st (:3100, :3251). The row prints one decimal (:3539), but the value is always an integer, so the only choices are 0 or at least 1 semitone of wobble.
- Fix: Drop the rounding and test it. Fractional pitch intensity already works elsewhere in the XML (Bend A at 0.1666667); that the matrix accepts it too is inferred. Use 0.01 st resolution with Shift-drag for fine moves, show cents below 1 st, and default new pitch routes to 0.15 st.

### B11. Dead and placeholder controls: REPEAT and +STACK, PREVIEW and strum offered in LOOPS, LFO 3 and 4 defaulting to uneditable STEPS and DRAWN shapes, and a hidden TRIP toggle

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified unless marked. SAMPLES draws REPEAT and +STACK as greyed pills that do nothing (SamplesPage.js:1341-1351). In LOOPS the PREVIEW pill is drawn (Interface.js:2113-2122) but only works in mode 0 (:2188); strum is gated the same way (:2190-2198), yet the cover text offers both (:2143). MOD offers DRAWN and STEPS (:3277) with no table or slider-pack editor. The XML already defaults LFO 3 to STEPS (WaveformType 7) and LFO 4 to DRAWN (6), with CustomWaveform="" and StepData "4......." (L119-136), so two of the four LFOs start on shapes the user cannot edit. Inferred from the JUCE MemoryBlock base64 format: that StepData is a single 0.0 step, so STEPS is flat (https://docs.juce.com/master/classMemoryBlock.html). STAGE TRIP only cycles when you click the 'STAGE / SHOW' text (:2166-2170), and it is not saved.
- Fix: Hide unbuilt controls or mark them 'coming'. In LOOPS, swap PREVIEW for a kit audition and fix the cover text. Default LFO 3 and 4 to sine or triangle until the ScriptSliderPack and ScriptTable editors ship (doc 16 Stage 3). Turn TRIP into a visible stepper backed by a saved control.

### B12. PLAY KEYS places 808 and bass one-shots below the Mac-key range and far above their own pitch on the visible piano

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified on index.json. For hits, smpSetPlayKeys (SamplesPage.js:668-675) sets Root rt, LoKey rt-24 and HiKey rt+24. The indexer roots 808 and bass sounds at pc+24..pc+36 (samples_index.py:129-160). 93 of 103 keyed 808/BASS one-shots have a root of 24-34, so HiKey is 48-58, below the Mac-key zone 60-76: A..K are silent. The lowest on-screen key, C2 (piano LowKey 36, Interface.js:2742), plays them 2 to 12 st above root. Doc 21b acceptance test 3 therefore needs an external controller or three presses of '['.
- Fix: Fold hit zones by octaves into roughly 48-72 and offset Root by the same octaves, as the loop branch already does with C3 = song key. Alternatively, show the playable range with a one-click octave button.

### B13. Loops that need more than 128 quarters play at the wrong tempo because of the NumQuarters clamp

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Counts verified on index.json. The tempo error is derived from code and not heard in Live. smpLoopNq = min(128, nq x feel x deviceRate / fileRate) (SamplesPage.js:475). The auto-feel guard (:116) covers the rate factor, but forced SMP_SPEED_M has no guard. Of 2,007 indexed loops, on a 48 kHz device 3 exceed 128 at 1x and 35 at 2x; on a 96 kHz device, 35 and 481. Example: a 128-quarter 44.1 kHz loop at 48 kHz, clamped to 128, runs about 8.8 percent fast and ends about 10.4 quarters early each cycle. The playhead (:1322) uses the unclamped length. The 128 limit belongs to the per-zone NumQuarters property (ModulatorSamplerSound.cpp:298, :723). TimestretchOptions::fromJSON does not clamp (ModulatorSampler.h:308-313), so the script's clamp may be self-imposed; the tempo_syncer limit was not verified.
- Fix: When the corrected value exceeds 128, halve the feel, or mark the row and skip it in IN TEMPO. Apply the same guard to forced speeds. After a Live probe, consider removing the clamp on the options value.

### B14. Backspace (previous sound) works once, then does nothing or skips a sound

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by trace; triage re-read SamplesPage.js:801-803. smpBack removes the current id (:801), plays hist[i] (:802), then removes the last element again (:803, commented 'smpPlayRes logged it again'). But smpNoteHistory only pushes when the last id differs, so :803 deletes the sound that just started. With hist [X, A, B], the first press plays A and leaves [X], and the second press does nothing. With [W, X, A, B], the second press jumps to W and skips X.
- Fix: Delete line 803.

### B15. SAMPLES counters print decimals: '4.0/2400' and '13.0-24.0 of 2,400'

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source. A Results click sets r = SM.top + Math.floor(...) (:1217-1218), a double, which smpPlayRes stores in SM.sel. smpEnsureVisible (:567) and smpRefilter (:439) set SM.top = Math.floor(...) * SMP_ROWS. The header prints (SM.sel + 1) + "/" (Interface.js:959). Results prints SM.top + 1 and Math.min(int, double) (:1128), and at 4.1.0 Math.min and Math.max return an int only when both arguments are ints (MathObject.cpp:192-196).
- Fix: Wrap the derived indices in Math.round (:1217, :439, :567, :1246), or format with Engine.doubleToString(v, 0). It shares its root cause with the FORM pill bug.

### B16. FIX KEY stores the song's MAJ/MIN as the sound's mode, which can make the key match worse

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source. The grid click calls smpFixKey(smpSelItem(), gk, SM.songMode) (:1041). While SM.grid is open, smpRailHit returns only g* and gclose (:866-875), so clicking MAJ/MIN just closes the grid. Example: in a C major song, an A minor loop fixed to A is stored as A major, so smpShift gives +3 and the loop plays in C minor. Stored as minor, the shift would be 0.
- Fix: Add MAJ / MIN / ? cells to the fix grid, defaulting to the item's current mode.

### B17. Shift+Backspace hides a sound forever: there is no unhide and no help text

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by grep. SM.hidden is set only at :781, saved at :165, restored at :179 and filtered at :351. CLEAR (:1076-1089) does not touch it, and the help text (:1015) lists only plain Backspace. A slip on Shift removes a sound from every future search, and the only way back is editing AppData SampleBrowser/user.json by hand.
- Fix: Add a HIDDEN chip or an UNHIDE ALL entry in the rail, a one-step undo of the last hide, and the shortcut in the help text.

### B18. In LOOPS the stage keeps the previously loaded instrument's look instead of the kit's

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source; a triage grep confirms loadKit (Interface.js:3768-3781) never calls buildVD. In LOOPS the look therefore comes from FACTORY_BANK[S.cur], so kits like Seance Tape or Omen Bells show whatever club rig the last instrument used.
- Fix: Call buildVD from the kit's tags in loadKit, with two caveats. First, buildVD reads p.cat, which kits do not have, so add a kit category fallback. Second, with the current SCENE_TAGS priority, Omen Bells (tag 808) would map to THUMP and Seance Tape (phonk) to the PHONK red-strobe rig, so the new world scenes (see gaps) must win over 808 and phonk. Keep flashOK (WCAG 2.3.1) for every scene.

### B19. The Splice indexer opens a WAL-mode database with immutable=1, so the newest downloads can lose key, BPM and form (personal-only tool)

- Severity: minor; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Mechanism verified; impact inferred. AppData tools/adapters/splice.py:20 opens "file:...?mode=ro&immutable=1". sounds.db-wal (8,272 bytes) and sounds.db-shm exist (only names and sizes were read). In a scratch test, 5 rows committed only to the WAL were visible with mode=ro and invisible with immutable=1. The SQLite docs say immutable skips locking and change detection (https://www.sqlite.org/uri.html). index_report shows 7 orphans; whether they are WAL rows is unknown.
- Fix: Copy sounds.db, -wal and -shm to a temp dir and open the copy. Plain mode=ro may write read-marks into -shm (inferred from https://www.sqlite.org/walformat.html), which would break the adapter's 'never writes' promise. Report the orphan count on every run. This adapter is personal-only and must never enter the public repo.

### B20. Results break past 8,192 sounds, and the indexer's similarity step needs O(n^2) memory

- Severity: low; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source. Latent: the index holds 2,400 sounds today. SMP_PACK = 8192 (:40), and keys are packed as sortKey * 8192 + i and unpacked with % 8192, with no length guard (:430-435): item 8,200 would be listed and played as item 8. samples_index.py:358-381 builds dense n x n matrices (some float32, some float64 or int64). At 20,000 items each takes 1.6 to 3.2 GB.
- Fix: Sort [key, index] pairs, or sort an index array with a comparator. Raising SMP_PACK to 16,384 is not safe, because scores up to 200,000 overflow int32. Compute similarity in row blocks with a running top-12 per row.

### B21. The licence CI gate knows one vendor, is case-sensitive and skips docs/ and design/

- Severity: low; status: confirmed
- Evidence: Verified by source. This is a gap, not a current leak: a case-insensitive grep over docs and design finds nothing today. scripts/ci/check_licence_boundary.py:15-16 has PATTERNS ["/Splice/", "sounds.db"], matched with case-sensitive git grep -F, and the scopes (:22) leave out docs/ and design/ even though docs/21b is tracked. Keyscape or Omnisphere paths, or an absolute /Users/ path in a SampleMaps XML, would pass.
- Fix: Use a data-driven deny list (vendor path fragments, library names, SampleBrowser) with git grep -i, add docs/ and design/ to the scopes, and fail on any FileName="/Users/ in SampleMaps. Any Keyscape or Omnisphere material is personal-only and never goes in the public repo.

## 2. Gaps against what was asked

- TRIAGE NOTE, install state resolved: the UX lane's 'build in Live is behind HEAD' finding is stale and was dropped from the bugs. A triage check shows installed_build.txt now reads run 43276764715, commit e94ae83, installed 2026-11-13 22:44, and both Sweet Melody.component and Sweet Melody.vst3 are dated 2026-11-13 22:44. Any 'verified in Live' result from before 22:44 ran on 60875b6. That includes the latch-visual and kit-pager fixes on the already-fixed list, which landed in f41a786 after the 60875b6 install. Re-run those checks and the doc 21b probes P1-P4 on e94ae83.
- Mystic texture and world (the user's core ask) is not in the engine. Root FX are Drive, ladder filter, Air EQ, Chorus, Delay and SimpleReverb, which HISE 4.1.0 SimpleReverb.h itself describes as Freeverb-based. There is one StreamingSampler plus a sine sub: no atmosphere layer, no Convolution, no ShapeFX, no ReleaseTrigger, and none of doc 13's Submerge or Tape stages. Reverb FreezeMode exists in the XML but has no control; that it works is inferred, so test it by ear before exposing it. Build order: (1) an Atmos StreamingSampler layer fed by the user's own field recordings and room tones; (2) Convolution on a parallel send, with IRs the user records; (3) a SUBMERGE macro (ShapeFX Reduce, filter, Air EQ); (4) a tape-wow LFO on A Pitch, which first needs the semitone-snap fix; (5) a FREEZE button.
- Sounds are static: 0 of 197 presets carry mod rows, only 2 use the filter envelope (rain_neon_brass, wet_wire_squelch), and 33 carry 'd' values, 19 of them DRIFT only. Plan a sound-design pass on 20-30 'mystic' presets first, with blind A/B against the current versions. Use slow LFO 1 or 2 (sine or triangle) to cutoff, Env 3 to level swells and filter-envelope plucks. For pitch instability, use the existing DRIFT (Voice Drift Random), not a matrix Random-to-pitch, while the pitch depth still snaps to semitones.
- Sampling depth is below the doc 13 bar. Across 264 maps (3,829 zones) there are no round robins, every SIG zone has UpperVelocityXFade 0, there are at most 2 velocity layers, and there are no release samples. All SIG zones have SampleStartMod 0, so a Random sample-start modulator alone does nothing: build_signature_bank.py must write a nonzero value first, and only for sustained pads, because start scatter would blunt pluck, nylon, 808 and bell transients. For percussive sounds, render 2-3 seeded round robins, 4 velocity layers with crossfades for keys and plucks, and later release layers from the user's own recordings.
- Instruments ready for melody inside LOOPS: impossible today. setMode bypasses Voice A and the Sub in every mode except PLAY, so only notes 60-75 sound in LOOPS. The 124 lane preset ids (all present in FactoryBank) are never shown; the UI only says KEYS/BASS/PLUCK/AIR. Prerequisites before splitting the keyboard: (a) a per-synth note filter as a child MIDI script, following SmpLoopsIn.js; (b) move the instrument's colour FX into Voice A's own chain, or give Loop Lines its own FX (its chain at XML L405 is empty). Without these, a 'PLAY THIS LANE' chip that calls loadPreset would re-darken the loops. Then show the lane preset names and add the chip.
- LOOPS has no song key. The 64 kits sit in 22 keys (A minor 6, F# minor 5, Db major 5, and so on), and no script calls setCoarseDetune, addPitchFade or setTransposeAmount. Reuse the path SAMPLES already ships: rewrite the zone Root (SamplesPage.js:650) and reload the map at a loop boundary or on kit load, since event detune reaching the stretcher is unverified (doc 15 L164). Shift modal kits ('C# pelog', 'A Phrygian dominant') by root only. Use one global SongKey shared by SAMPLES, LOOPS, keyboard tint and previews.
- LOOPS has no SNAP: SampleRouter only quantizes channel 15 (SAMPLES auditions), there is no LoopRouter.js, and KeyShift passes latched loop notes straight through. Hand-played or latched lanes stay as far off the bar, and off each other, as they were played; quantized Live clips are unaffected. Reuse the SampleRouter grid code with Snap LOOP/BAR/BEAT/OFF, keep setFixNoteOnAfterNoteOff(true), and show a 'queued' LED on waiting keys.
- There is no key or BPM awareness outside SAMPLES (doc 20). No Performer.js means no key or scale lock, FactoryBank has no 'key' field, and the header shows no host BPM or key. The PREVIEW phrases use fixed roots per category (PREV_ROOT: C, G, E, D) with a 92 BPM fallback, and the crowd strum is C minor pentatonic. Host parameters can only come from Interface controls, so KEY, SCALE and SNAP must be Interface knobs that forward to Performer.js.
- The Moog-style playing modes are missing: no POLY/MONO/LEGATO, no GLIDE (doc 16 Stage 4) and no arpeggiator (Stage 5). As a result long_glide_808, molasses_glide_sub, ladder_lead and fat_mono_line cannot slide as their names promise. Voicing.js must pass notes through untouched in LOOPS and SAMPLES, or mono note-stealing will end latched lanes.
- Doc 14's melody helpers are missing: one-finger chord colours, roll and timing humanize, MIDI out (VST3 only; doc 14 L69 says AU cannot send MIDI out in Live), TUNE +/-50 cents and SLOWED (ASemiKnob is whole semitones over -12..12; doc 14 puts slowed uploads at 0.72x-0.89x).
- There is no way to save your own sound. Add MY SOUNDS: a SAVE button that writes the base preset id, macros, A/R, DV/MV values and Matrix.toBase64() to AppData through File.writeObject (the API is already used at Interface.js:311 and SamplesPage.js:168), plus a USER pill in the browser. The 5 v0.1 'User Presets/Factory' folders are unreachable.
- Only 10 host parameters (8 macros plus Attack/Release). SHAPE (30 controls) and MOD (24) cannot be automated in Live. Blocking risk: the KEY/SCALE, Voicing, TUNE/SLOWED and doc 16 SHAPE items each say 'append at the end' but propose different orders. HISE assigns indices by creation order, and doc 14 L46 already reserves the next slots. Lock one order in a single commit, with a selfTest on parameter count and order, before adding any of them.
- There is no FX page (doc 17 page 5). Delay time comes only from the preset's dly field, PUMP is fixed at 1/4, and reverb size, damping and width only move as macro side effects. A page with DELAY TIME L/R by note value, PUMP RATE, REVERB SIZE/DAMP/FREEZE and AIR gains needs no new DSP.
- 'More loops' ask: loop content is thin. All 1,024 LL takes are 16-bit (chosen for disk, which now has about 99 GiB free). Velocity in loops is gain-only ((vel/127)^1.4), and the RNG seed is per note rather than per hit, so repeated notes reuse the same waveform. wood_bass_pluck is the BASS lane in 14 of 64 kits, 59 of 64 kits are 2 bars, and there are only 4 lanes with no texture or percussion lane. For new kits: 24-bit, per-hit seed and velocity layer, a 5th TEXTURE lane from the user's own recordings, 4-bar B sections, and no lane preset in more than about 5 kits.
- LOOPS discovery is thin. There are 64 kits at 7 per page (10 pages), the search field is hidden in LOOPS, there are no tag chips (257 distinct tags exist), no hearts and no kit preview. Kit switches cut playback because loadSampleMap kills voices, and there is no phase ring or 'HOST 145 / KIT 72 x2 feel' readout.
- Look and feel (Porcelain Lab, doc 17) is only half ported. The window is still 1024x700 while the tokens assume 1200x800, the header reads V0.2, and there is no status bar, tooltip panel (tooltip strings exist but are never shown) or zoom. T.MONO falls back to Oxygen because no OFL mono font is bundled, and the stage plate is the Pillow placeholder. sm_backdrop.png, sm_backdrop_dim.png, VuMeter.js and UserPresetWidgets.js are unused.
- The stage only shows a festival concert. Its 13 scenes and 7 rigs include nothing mystic, although kits are tagged mythical (6), eerie (3), ritual (2) and haunted (2). Add world scenes on the existing layer system (fog shrine, candle vigil, underwater temple, desert night) mapped to those tags, a VISUALS OFF toggle, and the doc 18b Stage 3 Blender plates, each render needing the user's OK.
- Copy undercuts the 'world' feeling. All 50 ARCHIVE presets read 'Original Research Facility lab specimen (...)', and 10 say '(basse)', which looks like a v0.1 folder name but reads as a typo. The macro card text is the same for every preset, and the LOOPS lower third is generic. Write a one-line scene and play tip per preset from doc 12, per-sound macro subtitles, and use kit tags in LOOPS.
- Vocal hook (doc 22) has zero code, and that is expected: doc 22 L97 puts it in a separate Hook Lab.app companion. The disk gate has cleared (99 GiB free versus 7.6 GiB at writing), but the other Stage 0 gates remain: private folders with Voices on an encrypted volume, a lawyer review of the voice release template, and the user's explicit approval of the Stage 1 downloads. Own voice or signed singers only; no artist cloning. The Tiger DiffSinger bank stays a private test only (CC BY-NC-ND 4.0 plus Commons Clause).
- Not covered by either audit lane: no one measured the bank's character against the user's named references (an xx-style sparse synth, Moog-style mono lines, rare piano synths) or the 61-track playlist in docs/12b. Gap analysis and recipes need the research lane, with primary sources for every gear claim. Any sampling of the user's own Keyscape or Omnisphere is personal-only and never goes in the public repo.
- There is no render QA gate: CI does not check DC offset, onset steps or loop-seam clicks, which is how the tine DC step shipped across 83 SIG files and several kits. Add a curvature-based click and seam metric, plus a render-wide 1-2 ms fade-in, before turning on the |x0| thresholds.

## 3. Sound audit A: keys, chords, pads, bells, guitar, plucks

### Method

Read-only audit of the 95 presets in keys (32), chords (12), pads (22), bells (10), guitar (7) and plucks (12). Recipes were dumped by importing scripts/build_signature_bank.py (PRESETS, which includes the Legends packs) to recipes.json. Nothing in the repo was written. Scripts and outputs are in /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-noxvitae/0e6ab598-c428-4549-a613-b529408ad567/scratchpad/wf_mystic/:
- audit_part_a.py writes audit_part_a.csv, audit_part_a.json and similar_pairs.csv.
- fault_scan.py writes fault_scan.csv.
- metric_strings.json holds the per-preset summaries.
Tools: Python 3.12, numpy 2.4.6, scipy 1.17.1 (scipy.io.wavfile, 24-bit read as int32 divided by 2^31).

MAIN FILE per preset: SIG_<id>_60, or the nearest zone. For vl=2 presets the hard layer _v2 was used, and _v1 was used for the velocity contrast.

METRICS:
- Level: peak, body RMS over 1 s from onset, crest.
- Spectral centroid: energy-weighted log mean over frames within 40 dB of max, after the attack.
- motion_db: 1/3-octave band levels per channel, smoothed over 0.25 s and linearly detrended per band, then std averaged with energy weights. It measures evolution that is NOT the plain decay; about 0 dB means frozen.
- slow_evolve_oct: detrended smoothed centroid range.
- Spectral flux.
- Harmonic analysis on a 32768-sample window with 4x zero-pad and peak picking:
  - tuning (fundamental peak, plus a plateau-free harmonic fit)
  - inharm_frac (peak energy more than 15 c off the harmonic grid)
  - stretch_c
  - ntr_db (non-peak vs peak energy)
  - floor_db (median inter-peak level)
  - interharm_db (mean level between harmonics k=1..8 vs at the harmonics). This is the texture/noise-floor number used in verdicts.
- Stereo: side/mid dB, L/R correlation, low-band (<200 Hz) correlation, mono loss.
- Pitch: wow from an 8192/2048 track of the strongest low harmonic (p2p and rate). Unreliable for unison saws, where it tracks the detune cluster.
- Envelope: attack (-20 to -1 dB), time to -20 dB, early and late slope, end_db (level just before the 40 ms render fade, re peak).
- Bands: air (>10 kHz share), low-mid 200-500 Hz share (dominated by the fundamental at C4, so not used for verdicts), sub.
- Faults: DC, first-sample step re peak, clip count.
- Loop seam for loop presets, using the builder's own loop_points(): RMS and 1/3-octave mismatch between [LS-xf, LS] and [LE-xf, LE], which are the two regions the sampler crossfades.
- Velocity: v2 minus v1, RMS and centroid.
- Keyboard sweep over every zone: level span, adjacent jump, worst end_db, tuning.
- Similarity: 0.6 x band-profile correlation + 0.4 x envelope similarity at C4. This is not perceptual, but the top pairs were cross-checked against identical recipes in code.

FAULT SCAN: fault_scan.py read all 1,932 zone/layer files of these presets for:
- note-on step
- DC
- clipping
- mono fold-down over the first 300 ms

Its second-difference click detector flags every saw/pulse period reset, so it was not used for clicks on saw, pulse or voice sources.

RUN TIME: about 40 s for the main audit and 27 s for the fault scan.

CAVEATS:
- I measured the dry rendered samples, not the plugin output. Live was not run, and runtime Drive, Master Filter, Air EQ, Chorus, Space Delay and SimpleReverb are not included. The module tree (XmlPresetBackups/ResearchFacility.xml) shows a single sampler voice A, a SineSynth sub, the loop samplers and that FX chain. There is no texture layer and no convolution, so the samples are the only source of noise, inharmonicity and stereo in the dry signal.
- Zone tuning outliers (bakelite 96, leaning 84/96, formant 78/84, tape_organ 90, noise pads) come from the fit landing on wow phase, noise or formant peaks. They are not tuning bugs. The clean tonal presets measured within about ±10 c, and lg_basement_drop (slow=0.5) within ±2 c.

SOURCES:
- The shared WebSearch budget was used up by other agents on the first call, so no new searches were possible. 'Verified' below means I fetched the URL and read the claim:
  - JOS Extended Karplus-Strong page (Jaffe and Smith 1983 extensions): https://ccrma.stanford.edu/~jos/pasp/Extended_Karplus_Strong_Algorithm.html
  - JOS Commuted Synthesis: https://ccrma.stanford.edu/~jos/pasp/Commuted_Synthesis.html
  - Huovilainen, DAFx 2004, Moog ladder with tanh per stage: https://dafx.de/paper-archive/2004/P_061.PDF
  - Chowning 1973 JAES 21:526-534, citation via https://ccrma.stanford.edu/software/snd/snd/fm.html
  - Rhodes Service Manual ch.4 Timbre Adjustment (tine end slightly above dead centre of pickup): https://www.fenderrhodes.com/org/manual/ch4.html
  - JOS nonlinear hammer felt, f = Q0 x^p with p = 3.7 + 0.015n: https://ccrma.stanford.edu/~jos/pasp/Nonlinear_Spring_Model.html
  - Paulstretch, extreme stretch such as 50x into textures: http://hypermammut.sourceforge.net/paulstretch/
- Producer facts quoted from docs/12 and docs/13 (40's Lo-Fi via SOS, Tainy 'dirty, nothing bright', Clams Casino reverse, the Song Exploder five takes, the HISE Chorus shared LFO and its rate formula) carry the sources listed in those docs and were not re-verified here.
- Physics values marked 'inferred' (cantilever ratios 6.27/17.55, bowl doublets) are design choices that were not checked against a source.

### Bank-wide patterns

The bank is too clean, too static, too mono and too samey. It also has four real bugs. All numbers are across the 95 part-A presets; the 'verified' items were checked in code or by measurement.

BUGS
1. Truncated tails, verified. 26 of 61 one-shot presets are cut while still louder than -40 dB re peak at their worst zone, and 14 of them are louder than -30 dB. The worst: pixel_bell -15, leaning_upright -16, bakelite_upright -16, lg_basement_drop -17, hushed_felt_grand -17.5, moonlit_upright -18, prepared_bolt_piano -19, submerged_felt -20 dB. Non-looped samples stop at their end with a 40 ms fade, so held chords and long releases die. The playlist is mostly half-time (docs/12), so this hits hardest exactly where it matters. Fix F1: dur up to 12 s (the builder's max), sized to the lowest zone's t60.
2. Mono fold-down, verified in gen_modal and gen_glass_pad. The random start phase is drawn separately for L and R, so 13 presets have zones losing more than 6 dB when summed to mono in the first 300 ms. Worst: dusty_kalimba -16.5 (L/R corr -0.87), tropic_mallet -16.3, night_marimba -13.6, music_box_memory -12.3, eight_hertz -12.2, night_celesta -12.0, lg_steel_lotus -11.6 (8 of 40 zones). Fix F2: one phase per mode, shared by L and R, keeping the ±beat_c detune.
3. Note-on steps, verified. 12 presets start at 5% to 24% of peak. Causes:
   - tine_ep subtracts the mean of its first 2048 samples and then high-passes.
   - gen_prepared adds metal and rattle after the attack ramp.
   - pulse DC is removed by render_voice's whole-file mean after the envelope.
   Exposed under ATTACK 1 to 5 ms: perreo_keys, prepared_bolt_piano, velvet_tines, far_apart_duo, dusk_poly_chords, lg_bark_stage, glass_room_ep, saloon_tack_piano. Fix F3: a 2 ms raised-cosine fade-in after mean removal, plus the ramp in gen_prepared.
4. Loop seams, verified by measurement. 15 of 34 looped presets mismatch by more than 1.5 dB in level or more than 4 dB in band profile at the crossfade. Worst: cold_glass +4.8 dB, formant_night_pad -4.4 dB with 10.2 dB band mismatch (the vowel morph spans the whole render), midnight_wash -3.4, brass_memory_poly +3.0. The median loop is only 2.97 s, so every pad visibly repeats. Fix F5: loop after the envelope settles, dur 10 to 12, baked motion in whole cycles of the loop or moved to runtime.

WOW
The wow is baked per zone with a random phase (sigbank_dsp.wow, verified). Every note in a chord drifts on its own, by 10 to 16 c p2p on the underwater keys. It reads as detuned chorus, not one tape (perceptual effect inferred). Fix F4: render without wow and drive one shared runtime LFO to A Pitch through the mod matrix. Whether the global LFOs are shared across voices in HISE 4.1.0 is inferred, not checked.

TOO CLEAN (no noise floor, no world)
- The median inter-harmonic level is -97 dB, and 49 of 95 are below -90 dB. There is effectively nothing between the partials.
- 68 of 95 have an inter-peak floor below -100 dB re max bin.
- Only tape, breath, voice, decim, organ-click and prepared presets carry texture.
- Calibration on the same felt generator: tape hiss 0.003 gives -71 dB (leaning_upright) against -115 dB without it (moonlit_upright).
- 55 of 95 have less than -60 dB of energy above 10 kHz (median -67 dB), so there is no air for SPACE or TONE to open up.
- docs/13 records that Frank Dukes keeps the synths' noise floor (SOS). The bank does the opposite.
Fix T1: tape={'hiss':0.0015 to 0.004, 'bump', 'sat'} on most keys and bells.

TOO STATIC
- 37 of 95 have spectral motion below 0.5 dB, frozen apart from the decay. That includes 10 of 11 tine EPs and every modal bell (0.00 dB).
- No preset in this lane uses the mod matrix.
- Only rain_neon_brass uses the filter envelope.
- 21 presets set any SHAPE value, and 17 of those set only DRIFT.
- Velocity: 52 of 95 have two layers, but the median timbre change between them is 0.13 octave of centroid. tropic_mallet, night_celesta, lg_rainy_tine, lg_highland_ten and bakelite_upright are essentially the same sound louder. pump_saw_stab, spring_surf_strum and lg_steel_lotus are inverted (the soft layer is brighter).

TOO MONO
- 23 of 95 are exactly mono in the sample: all 11 tine_ep, all 4 reed_ep, karplus without chorus, seven_sun_saw (spread 0), kaleido and coastline (1 voice), vox_chop_pluck.
- docs/13 records that the HISE 4.1.0 Chorus uses one LFO for both channels (not re-verified here), so the dry signal of these stays centred.
Fix T2: a stereo render per generator.

TOO SAMEY
- Generator monoculture: 27 of 95 are gen_supersaw (13 pads, 11 chords, 3 plucks), and 11 of 32 keys are the same 2-operator tine.
- 19 pairs have similarity of 0.97 or more. Identical recipes confirmed in code: tropic_mallet = night_marimba (0.999) and porch_nylon = dembow_nylon.
- Near-twins:
  - syrup_tine / glass_room_ep 0.982
  - lounge_drawbar_organ / tape_organ 0.984
  - wobble_reed_keys / hazy_reed_keys 0.978
  - drowned_tines / night_drive_keys / lg_low_end_chop 0.975 to 0.976
  - ensemble_poly_six / far_apart_duo / kaleido_sweep_poly 0.965 to 0.985
- 11 retirements are proposed, each with a rare or mystic replacement recipe:
  - Lullaby Underwater
  - Backward Tines
  - Fork Piano
  - Strip Choir
  - Dust Harmonium
  - Ladder Poly (the Moog ladder, Huovilainen DAFx 2004, verified)
  - Singing Bowl Field
  - Moog Drone Pad
  - Bowed Metal
  - Muted Tresillo Nylon
  - Bowed Bar

WHAT IS FINE
- Loudness is matched (body RMS -19.9 to -17.1 dBFS).
- No clipping; DC at most 1.1e-3.
- Tuning is within about ±10 c across zones for tonal generators.
- The slowed-tape path is in tune.

ENGINE GAPS BEHIND ALL OF THIS
- One sampler voice plus a sine sub. There is no second texture layer and no convolution (module tree).
- Proposal T3: a 'World' sampler layer under its own macro, holding the user's own recorded room tone, rain, street, vinyl and wind-up mechanisms, plus Paulstretch-style extreme stretches of their own recordings (verified source). cassette_hiss_bed and static_bloom are seed content.
- Other new generator features (each needs DSP code):
  - commuted body IR for guitars (JOS, verified)
  - EKS pick-direction, dynamic-level and allpass tuning (JOS, verified)
  - tanh ladder (verified)
  - PWM
  - per-voice vibrato jitter (gen_supersaw shares one vibrato across all voices, verified in code)
  - bow noise
  - pedal resonance
  - sustaining reed
- Personal-only third-party sources (the user's own Keyscape or Omnisphere, privately sampled) go in an SM_Personal expansion, never in the public repo.

OTHER
- build_signature_bank.py defines write_wav16 twice (lines 782 and 868). The second, with a different dither scale, silently replaces the first; low impact.
- 'xx synth' in the brief is ambiguous: The xx is not among the 61 playlist tracks, and no gear claims were made about it.
- Rare piano-synth roles already present and worth fixing first: stage_electric_grand, saloon_tack_piano, prepared_bolt_piano, funk_pick_clav, toy_box_piano, sticky_reed_keys (needs a real plucked-reed generator).

| lg_barrio_pluck | keep | n60; cent 437Hz; motion 1.06dB; interharm -107dB; S/M -1.0dB corr 0.13; monofold -5.3dB; air -45dB; crest 15.4dB; loop 1.5s seam +0.6dB/3.3; note-on step 5%pk@81; vel dCent 0.16oct; nearest lg_clank_house 0.925 | A loopable pulse pluck with motion and a clean seam. | Keep. F3. spread down to 0.6. |
| lg_steel_lotus | rework | n60; cent 269Hz; motion 0.00dB; interharm -56dB; S/M -5.4dB corr 0.56; monofold -11.6dB (8/40 zones); air -123dB; crest 12.0dB; end -59dB@50 (dur 4.5s); vel dCent -0.38oct (inverted); nearest lg_glass_ballad 0.911 | Steel-tongue style modal with the most mono failures in the bank: 8 of 40 zones lose more than 6 dB. Frozen, and the soft layer is brighter than the hard one. | F2. strike 0.3 to 0.45 for a mallet. Beat doublets on mode 2 (1.995 / 2.005) for a living steel tone. Fix the velocity inversion: soften must lower lp, not just strike. |

### Fact-check (18 items checked by an adversarial verifier)

- **CONFIRMED**: BUG 1: truncated one-shot tails (26 of 61 one-shot presets end louder than -40 dB re peak; 14 louder than -30 dB; worst pixel_bell -15). I re-scanned every zone and layer of all 61 one-shots (verify_sounds_a/allscan.py: 50 ms RMS just before the 40 ms render fade, re the peak 50 ms RMS). 26 are louder than -40 dB, which matches. 19 are louder than -30 dB, not 14; the count depends on the window. The worst zone is hushed_felt_grand_40_v1 at -11.3 dB, not pixel_bell. pixel_bell_48_v2 measures -14.8 dB, but its d sets AmpDecKnob 900 and AmpSusKnob -10.5, so the cut actually heard is about -25 dB re peak: still audible, though milder than reported. The same applies to lg_clank_house (AmpSus -12, end -23.7). The Amp Env default is Sustain 0 dB (deep_params.json), so the other 24 presets have nothing masking the cut. The code path is verified: render_preset applies a 40 ms end fade to non-looped samples. The pack builder accepts dur up to 12 s.
- **CONFIRMED**: BUG 2: mono fold-down in gen_modal / gen_glass_pad (13 presets lose more than 6 dB in mono over the first 300 ms; dusty_kalimba -16.5, corr -0.87; eight_hertz side 9.5 dB over mid). Verified in code: gen_modal and gen_glass_pad call rng.uniform(0, 6.28) separately for the L and R partial of each mode. My scan finds exactly 13 presets (11 modal, ghost_swarm_bed on glass_pad, and far_apart_duo on supersaw). These figures reproduced: dusty_kalimba_60 -16.5 dB (S/M +11.7, corr -0.87), tropic_mallet_66_v2 -16.3, eight_hertz worst -12.1 with 6 of 22 zones, night_celesta -12.0 with 6 of 18, music_box_memory -12.3 with 3 of 8. lg_steel_lotus has 7 of 40 zones below -6 dB, not 8.
- **REFUTED**: F2 prescription: one start phase per mode shared by L and R, keeping the +/-beat_c L/R detune. I simulated it (verify_sounds_a/f2sim.py, 12 seeds per recipe). F2 fixes the onset: dusty_kalimba's worst 0-300 ms fold goes from -16.2 to -0.2 dB, and eight_hertz from -7.6 to -0.1. But L and R remain detuned by 2 x beat_c, so the mono sum still cancels later at t = 1/(2 df). The worst 100 ms window in the first 1.5 s is still -27.5 dB for dusty_kalimba, -14.9 for toy_box_piano and -6.3 for first_light_bellpad. F2 also collapses onset width: L/R correlation over 0-300 ms becomes 0.93 (dusty) and 0.98 (eight_hertz), so these bells start almost mono. All the stereo width in gen_modal currently comes from the random phases. Correction: Make stereo mono-safe instead. Use the same phase per mode in L and R and pan each mode, or each doublet member, to its own position with equal-power gains. Put both detuned members of a beating pair into BOTH channels, cross-panned. The beating then becomes amplitude beating that survives mono, and the mono sum can never cancel a mode. gen_glass_pad needs the same treatment (its L/R +/-3 c detune plus independent phases).
- **CONFIRMED**: BUG 3: note-on steps (12 presets start at 5% to 24% of peak; causes: tine_ep 2048-sample mean subtraction, gen_prepared metal/rattle after the attack ramp, pulse DC removed by render_voice's whole-file mean). Verified in code and in the files. tine_ep runs x - mean(x[:2048]) before hp(30). gen_prepared adds metal (sin with random phase) and gated rattle noise after gen_felt_piano's ramp. render_voice returns l - mean(l), so any net DC becomes a constant offset at sample 0. Worst zones: perreo_keys_36 23.7%, prepared_bolt_piano_36_v2 23.5%, velvet_tines_36 23.0%, far_apart_duo 17.4%, dusk_poly_chords 14.7%. My scan counts 13 presets at 5% or more (lg_barrio_pluck 5.0% and optical_disc_organ 5.2% sit on the threshold). Runtime severity is not verified. The plugin's Amp Env AHDSR (module tree, AttackLevel 0 dB, 1 to 8 ms ATTACK on these presets) ramps up from silence, so the plugin output has no sample-level discontinuity. What survives is a short low-frequency DC thump (it decays over about 5 ms through the 30 Hz HP in tine_ep, and lasts for the attack in the pulse case). This inference is from standard AHDSR behaviour and was not measured in Live.
- **REFUTED**: Loop seams: cold_glass '+4.8 dB every 2.9 s, biggest level seam in the bank'; ensemble_poly_six '+2.3 dB'. audit_part_a.py measures seam_level_db on the mono (mid) sum, and only at the C4 file. Measured as stereo power (L^2+R^2) at the map's own LoopStart/LoopEnd/XFade (verify_sounds_a/seam2.py), cold_glass is median +0.4 dB and worst -2.2 dB over 9 zones. ensemble_poly_six is within 0.5 dB over 22 zones. Their mid-sum swings (cold_glass up to -12 dB) come from the L/R detune and independent phases (BUG 2) beating in mono. They are not loop seams. Correction: Re-file cold_glass under BUG 2 (mono fold) and drop its seam-driven 'rework' basis. Same for ensemble_poly_six's seam: its retire case rests on similarity alone. Measure seams in stereo power across all zones.
- **REFUTED**: frozen_halo verdict 'keep' (seam +0.8 dB at C4, 'the only flaw is the vowel-morph seam'). Across all zones, the stereo-power seam is -17.8 dB at zone 66, +12.1 dB at 72 and +8.5 dB at 78: the worst in the bank. The level envelope of frozen_halo_72 climbs from about -16 to -4 dB across the 6 s render. In zone 66 it falls about 15 dB. Cause, inferred from code: _formant_filter uses fixed-frequency bandpass formants with Q = f/bw, morphed over linspace(0, n-1), and at high pitches the sparse harmonics move in and out of the narrow formant peaks as the vowels slide. Keyboard gain is set from the first 1 s only, so these later swings are not normalised. Correction: Verdict should be 'rework'. Apply the loop fix (below) and cap the formant Q, or widen the bandwidth with f0, in the high zones. Also normalise on the loop body, not just the first second. Other worst-zone stereo seams the C4-only method missed: seven_sun_saw -5.6, supersaw_bloom +4.8 (verdict keep), anthem_brass_stab -4.3, shoegaze_haze_wall +3.9, brass_memory_poly +3.9.
- **CONFIRMED**: formant_night_pad seam (-4.4 dB / 10.2 dB band mismatch) and F5 'palindrome vowels [u,o,a,o,u] confined to the loop', 'loop start after 1.6 s'. The seam is real and worse than reported. Stereo power is median -2.3 dB, and zone 78 reaches +13.0 dB (it ramps from -20 to -1 dB). Verified in code: the vowel morph points are np.linspace(0, n-1, nv), so the morph always spans the whole render. The prescription as written does not work. With dur 12 and loop_points' ls = max(attack + 0.6, 1.0) = 1.6 s, the palindrome puts loop start about halfway along u to o and loop end at u, so the two loop edges still differ. 'Loop start after X s' is not a recipe field either: loop_points reads only gp['attack'].
- **REFUTED**: Velocity inversions: pump_saw_stab (-0.16 oct, 'fix with soften fenv 0.5'), lg_steel_lotus (-0.38 oct, 'soften must lower lp'), spring_surf_strum (-0.11 oct). pump_saw_stab: SOFT_RULES already scales supersaw fenv by 0.5 and lp by 0.65. The prescription is a no-op, and I confirmed the disk files match a fresh render (corr 1.0). The hard layer has more top: the share above H8 is -20.4 dB against -32.4 dB for soft. The log centroid is skewed by a resonance bump near H3 in the soft layer (cutoff 780 Hz). lg_steel_lotus: the power-spectrum centroid difference is 0.00 oct at zones 60, 62 and 66. The -0.38 oct is a mid-sum cancellation artefact (BUG 2), and soften already lowers lp to 0.7x. spring_surf_strum: the hard layer is 5 dB brighter above H8. The layers differ mainly because gen_karplus excites with ONE period of seeded noise, so the harmonic balance is random per seed: H3 is -13.4 dB in v1 and -4.7 dB in v2 at C4. Correction: Replace 'inverted' with these findings: lg_steel_lotus has no velocity timbre contrast (fix with a strike or mode-amplitude contrast, not lp); pump_saw_stab is fine. New plausible issue for all karplus presets: random per-zone and per-layer harmonic balance. In porch_nylon_v2, adjacent zones differ by a median 13 dB on single harmonics among H1 to H6 (verify_sounds_a/kszones.py; left channel, so partly body and comb). Use a multi-period or shaped excitation for consistency.
- **REFUTED**: Recipe-format fit of the 'vl 2: soft X / hard Y' prescriptions (drowned_tines bark 0.4/1.3, syrup_tine 0.6/2.4, wobble_reed drive 0.8/1.8, hushed hardness 0.15/0.4, prepared metal_mix 0.3/0.6, etc.). Verified in build_signature_bank.py. The soft layer is always soften(gen, gp): fixed per-generator multipliers (tine_ep bark x0.55, felt_piano hardness x0.5, reed_ep drive x0.7, prepared metal_mix x0.7, hardness x0.5). There is no per-preset override, and load_instrument_packs rejects unknown top-level keys. 'Soft 0.6 / hard 2.4' (a 0.25 ratio) cannot be expressed. Adding vl 2 to a vl 1 preset also doubles its files. Correction: Either restate these as hard values that soften() maps (for example hard bark 2.4 gives soft 1.32), or first add a gp['soft'] override dict to soften(). gp is free-form, so that passes pack validation.
- **REFUTED**: 'Absorb as a snapshot' / 'point at another preset's sample map' (night_drive_keys, tape_organ, hazy_reed_keys, dembow_nylon, dotted_echo_arp, pump_saw_stab and supersaw_bloom detune snapshots). bank_records() hard-codes map=f'SIG_{id}' for every preset, and RECIPE_KEYS has no map or source field. A snapshot that reuses another preset's samples cannot be written in the current format. Note also that dotted_echo_arp is not the same source as chorus_jangle_clean: t60 2.0 vs 2.4, bright 5500 vs 5000, body 0.3 vs 0.35, post_shelf only on jangle, zone step 4 vs 3. A 'detune 12 snapshot' changes the render, so it is a new preset, not a snapshot. Correction: This needs a builder change first: an optional 'map' key that skips rendering and points at an existing SIG map, added to RECIPE_KEYS and bank_records. Only then do the retire-into-snapshot plans work as described.
- **REFUTED**: T1 'tape hiss' prescriptions written as tape={'hiss':x} or 'T1 hiss 0.001' (crystal_fm_bell, stage_electric_grand, lg_glass_ballad, digital_glass_ep, celeste_mist, Backward Tines tape={'hiss':0.003,'lp':6000}, bakelite tape without wow_c). Verified in tape_age: unspecified keys take defaults of wow_c 8, flutter_c 3 at 9 Hz (random phase per zone), lp 7000, bump 0.25, sat 1.2. A bare hiss entry would add 8 c random-phase wow (the F4 problem) and a 7 kHz low-pass, which would further darken the bells the audit says lack air. lg_bark_stage shows the safe form: wow_c 0, flutter_c 0, lp 18000, sat 1.0. Inferred: baked hiss sums with polyphony (about +6 dB for 4 notes, +9 dB for 8) and is cut by the 40 ms sample-end fade. Correction: Spell out every key (wow_c 0, flutter_c 0, bump 0, sat 1.0, lp 20000) when only hiss is wanted. Better still, put noise and crackle in a runtime World/noise layer (the proposed T3, or a HISE NoiseSynth) so they do not stack per voice or gate with each sample.
- **UNCERTAIN**: F4 for drowned_tines (gp wow 0, mod LFO 1 -> A Pitch 0.05 Bipolar, d DriftKnob 2) and the 'per-zone wow phase makes chords seasick' argument. Verified: tine_ep's wow phase comes from the per-zone seeded rng. The mod row passes check_mod (A Pitch range is +/-12 semitones, so 0.05 is 5 c), and LFO 1 is a global time-variant LFO in 'Mod Sources', so all voices share it. Missed: DriftKnob drives 'Voice Drift', a HISE Random pitch modulator applied at voice start (static per note), whose default is 7.2 c (Interface.js line 561, deep_params.json). 76 of 95 part-A presets use that default, so at runtime every chord note already gets a random offset within +/-7.2 c, larger than the 5 c baked wow. The auditor's '+/-2 c in tune' figures for pianos are dry-sample only. Setting DriftKnob 2 REDUCES detune; it does not add drift. LFO rate cannot be set per preset: applyPresetMod resets LFO 1 to its defaults (tempo-synced, rate index round(0.2778*18) = 5). Correction: Keep F4, but describe DriftKnob as a per-note static detune and lower it on pianos and bells too (hushed_felt_grand, stage_electric_grand and the bells all run at 7.2 c now). Whether chords sound out of tune is an inferred listening question; test by ear. If the wow needs a specific Hz, add an LFO-rate field to the format.
- **REFUTED**: Mod rows targeting Cutoff without SHAPE cutoff (midnight_wash LFO 4 -> Cutoff 0.25; seven_sun_saw Aftertouch -> Cutoff Scale 0.4). The 'Cutoff' matrix target is the per-voice 'Voice Filter' (Interface.js line 3209). Its CutoffKnob defaults to 20000 Hz, and updateVoiceFilter bypasses it while idle. Without d CutoffKnob, a modulated filter at around 20 kHz will be nearly inaudible (inferred from the module defaults). kaleido's prescription does set CutoffKnob 500, so it is fine. No factory preset uses mod rows today (0 of 197 in FactoryBank.js), so this path is untested in factory content. Correction: Add d {'FTypeKnob':0 or 1, 'CutoffKnob':about 1500 to 4000} to any recipe with a Cutoff row, and audition in Live before shipping.
- **REFUTED**: kaleido_sweep_poly 'named sweep but no sweep (0.000 oct)'. True only for the dry sample. At runtime, MOTION 0.6 sets the Movement LFO (bipolar, on the Master Filter at TONE 1600 Hz) to intensity 0.18 at the preset's lfo of 0.15 Hz, and it also raises the Chorus rate and feedback (Interface.js lines 443 to 450, module tree). The preset desc says it is 'printed dry so a phaser and chorus can swirl it'. Mono from 1 voice is intentional. Correction: Treat the ladder/LFO 3 prescription as an optional enhancement, not a defect fix.
- **REFUTED**: dusk_poly_chords: 'set the HISE Chorus to Rate 0.566 (0.5 Hz)'. Recipes cannot set the Chorus rate directly; MOTION sets it as 0.392 + 0.343*m[2] (Interface.js). dusk's MOTION 0.4 gives Rate 0.529, about 0.39 Hz by docs/13's formula 10^(3*Rate - 2). The 0.566 figure itself checks out (it gives 0.499 Hz). Correction: Set m[2] to about 0.51 to reach Rate 0.566. Note this also raises the Movement LFO depth to 0.15 and the chorus feedback.
- **REFUTED**: rain_neon_brass is 'the only preset in the bank that uses the filter envelope'. FactoryBank.js has two presets with FEnvAmtKnob: rain_neon_brass 0.55 and wet_wire_squelch 0.85. Correction: 'The only part-A preset using the filter envelope.'
- **UNCERTAIN**: Twin/duplicate claims (tropic_mallet = night_marimba 0.999; porch_nylon = dembow_nylon; velvet_tines = lg_bark_stage minus tape; syrup_tine twin of glass_room_ep 0.982; night_drive_keys differs from drowned_tines 'only by TONE 950 and decim 8000'). Recipe dumps (verify_sounds_a/dump.py) confirm the first three. tropic and night_marimba have identical gp, z and dur. porch and dembow have identical gp. velvet_tines and lg_bark_stage share bark/tine/pickup with t60 6.5 vs 7.0. syrup_tine vs glass_room_ep is overstated: tine 0.22@7.11 vs 0.6@8.3, pickup 0.45 vs 0.2, bark 1.3 vs 0.8, lp 4500 vs none, TONE 3500 vs 8000. The 0.982 score reflects their near-sine sustains, not identical recipes. night_drive_keys also differs in bark (1.0 vs 0.9), tine, wow (6 vs 5), lp (2600 vs 2200), decim_drive (0.15 vs 0.08), and has no post_hp. Correction: Keep the first three as true duplicates. Re-justify retiring glass_room_ep on role and mono/static grounds, not on twin status, and correct the night_drive_keys diff list.
- **CONFIRMED**: Source claims: JOS felt law p = 3.7 + 0.015n; Rhodes manual ch.4 tine 'slightly above dead center'; Huovilainen DAFx 2004 tanh per stage; EKS filters (Jaffe and Smith 1983); cantilever 6.27/17.55 and free-bar 2.76/5.40/8.93 ratios 'inferred'. Verified by fetching the pages. JOS Nonlinear_Spring_Model gives p = 3.7 + 0.015n and Q0 = 183 e^(0.045n), citing Stulov, for key number n. The Rhodes ch.4 page says 'on a plane slightly above dead center of the Pickup'. The Huovilainen PDF has tanh in each stage (eqs 13 to 17) and says warmth 'is determined by the input amplitude'. The JOS EKS page lists six filters, including pick-direction, dynamic-level and string-tuning allpass, attributed to Jaffe and Smith 1983. The mode ratios check out mathematically: solving cos(x)cosh(x) = -1 gives 1 : 6.267 : 17.547, and = +1 gives 1 : 2.757 : 5.404 : 8.933 (Euler-Bernoulli uniform beams, not measured instruments).

## 4. Sound audit B: leads, vox, bass, archive

### Method

Read-only audit of the 102 part-B presets (leads 20, vox 13, bass 19, archive 50). Scripts and CSVs are in /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-noxvitae/0e6ab598-c428-4549-a613-b529408ad567/scratchpad/wf_mystic/: partb_metrics.py writes partb_metrics.csv (per preset) and partb_zones.csv (every hard-layer zone). partb_extra.py writes partb_loops.csv (loop seams on every looped zone), partb_loudness.csv, partb_dupes.csv and partb_zone_seams.csv. Also tickcheck.py (62.5 ms tick test), dupcheck.py (archive waveform correlation), envcheck.py and dccheck.py. Run with /Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.12/bin/python3, numpy/scipy only, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1.

FILE CHOICE: the hard (top) velocity layer at root 60 for leads/vox and 36 for bass, or the nearest root if missing (tinnitus_siren and wet_rim_seraph 72, altiplano_breath 66, angel_stretch_cloud and eight_second 61, wood_bass_pluck 37). Archive presets use their RF_*.wav at the map root.

METRICS:
- RMS, peak and crest: first 1 s after onset, the same window the builder uses.
- Centroid: median and CV over STFT frames (4096/512; bass 8192/1024), gated to -30 dB and starting 150 ms after onset (20 ms for sounds that fall 20 dB within 0.5 s).
- Spectral flux: mean positive L2 difference between L2-normalised magnitude frames.
- Harmonic analysis: a 0.5 s Hann chunk (1 s for bass, 0.25 s for short sounds), zero-padded 4x. NTR = non-harmonic to harmonic energy, using +-3% masks on k x f0 (an f0/2 grid when a sub-octave is within 25 dB). Inharmonicity = amplitude-weighted cents of the 16 strongest peaks below 8 kHz from the nearest harmonic. Tune = measured f0 against the map root.
- Drift: std and 5-95% range of per-frame cents on the strongest of harmonics 1-4, from 250 ms on. It includes in-sample vibrato and is unreliable for noise sources.
- Stereo: L/R correlation, side/mid ratio, mono fold-down loss, and correlation below 150 Hz for bass.
- Band shares (Welch): air above 10 kHz, mud 200-500 Hz, sub below 80 Hz, phone band 300-4000 Hz.
- Decay: time from peak to -20 and -40 dB on a 10 ms envelope.
- Clicks and DC: 1 ms frames of energy above 6 kHz that sit 12 dB over the local 100 ms 90th percentile; first and last sample level; DC; share of samples above the builder's 0.6 soft-limiter knee.
- Loop seams: exact simulation of HISE's crossfade, read from the local HISE clone (develop 6446c4a; StreamingSamplerSound.cpp rebuildCrossfadeBuffer and hlac SampleBuffer.h applyGainRampWithGamma; assumed unchanged in 4.1.0). HISE fades [ls-xf, ls) in over [le-xf, le) with gain = pow(ramp, CrossfadeGamma), and every map uses gamma 1.0. Reported per preset: worst 10 ms dip against the mean level of the two windows, and the level step between the 100 ms before and after the seam, over all looped hard-layer zones and both channels.
- Velocity: level and centroid ratio, hard layer over soft layer.
- Zone seams: centroid jump in semitones at each zone boundary with both neighbours resampled to the boundary.
- Loudness: max 400 ms RMS including preset trim. Unweighted plus a rough K-weighting proxy, not BS.1770.
- Duplicates: 1/3-octave spectrum distance under 3 dB and 3 s envelope distance under 4 dB at the same root.
- Root causes: read in scripts/sigbank_dsp.py, scripts/build_signature_bank.py and scripts/generate_library.py.

Verified means measured, read in code, or read in the source. Inferred means reasoned but not confirmed.

SOURCES:
The shared web-search budget ran out during research, so some sources below were seen only as search excerpts and are marked that way.

Verified (read):
- Moog ladder patent US3475623: https://patents.google.com/patent/US3475623A/en
- Huovilainen, DAFx-04: https://dafx.de/paper-archive/2004/P_061.PDF (tanh in every stage, 5 tanh per sample, 2x oversampling, half-sample delay in the feedback path).
- Werner, Abel and Smith, DAFx-14, TR-808 bass drum: https://dafx14.fau.de/papers/dafx14_kurt_james_werner_a_physically_informed,_ci.pdf (pages 1-2: the envelope generator shifts the bridged-T centre frequency in the first ms; leakage through the retriggering pulse gives the pitch its 'sigh'; a multi-second decaying pseudo-sinusoid).
- Rossing, ASA summary: https://acoustics.org/pressroom/httpdocs/147th/Rossing_Harmonicas1.htm (a wet finger excites the (2,0) mode and its harmonics by stick-slip). Paper: JASA 95(2):1106, 1994, doi 10.1121/1.408458.
- Sound On Sound, 'Recording The xx': https://www.soundonsound.com/people/rodaidh-mcdonald-recording-xx

Excerpt only, treat as inferred until read in full:
- Bloothooft et al. 1992, JASA 92:1827, overtone singing (formant clustering, narrow resonance): https://audiufon.hum.uu.nl/data/e_boventoon.html
- Hirschberg et al. 1996, JASA 99:1754, doi 10.1121/1.414698 (shock waves give trombones their brassiness): https://www.ioa.org.uk/system/files/proceedings/dm_campbell_why_do_brass_instruments_sound_brassy.pdf
- Ternstrom 2002, TMH-QPSR 43(1) (choir pitch scatter typically 20-30 cents): https://www.speech.kth.se/annualreport/2002/57-60tmhabstr.pdf
- Prame 1994, JASA 96:1979 (singer vibrato 5-7 Hz, as cited in a review).
- Ondes Martenot diffuseurs (Palme: 12 chromatic sympathetic strings; Metallique: gong diaphragm): https://dael.euracoustics.org/confs/fa2023/data/articles/000604.pdf
- Cristal Baschet (wet-finger friction; 'whiskers' emphasise higher harmonics): https://acta-acustica.edpsciences.org/10.1051/aacus/2023041
- Subharmonicon and Mixtur-Trautonium (secondary source): https://cdm.link/2020/05/moog-subharmonicon-first-look-plus-interview/
- Peterson and Barney 1952, doi 10.1121/1.1906875. That the gen_voice formant values match their male averages is inferred; the table was not read.

Repo docs/13 is cited as the repo's own record (40's Lo-Fi at 4400 Hz with a 95 Hz dip, attributed there to Sound On Sound and Mix).

### Bank-wide patterns

1) LOOP SEAMS. Verified; this is the biggest playability bug for melodies. 25 of 33 looped SIG presets in this lane have a worst-zone crossfade dip deeper than -6 dB, and 24 have a median dip of -4 dB or worse. A linear crossfade over uncorrelated material dips only about 3 dB, so anything deeper means the two windows are cancelling.
- Worst cases: tinnitus_siren -30.6 dB, twin_filter_scream -26.8, halo_smear -22.9, lowland_growl_bass -22.7, angel_stretch_cloud -20.6, ooh_ensemble -20.5, hollow_flute -20.0, helium_chop -17.3, ladder_mono_bass -16.3, lcr_ooh_choir -15.5 (median -11.9), boogie_keytar -14.9, formant_swirl -14.5 (with +16.4 dB level steps).
- Cause: loop_points() in build_signature_bank.py uses fixed times (ls = max(attack+0.6, 1.0) s, le = end - 30 ms, xf up to 0.35 s) and snaps to whole periods only for gen sub and reese. HISE fades [ls-xf, ls) in over [le-xf, le) with gain = pow(ramp, gamma), and every map has CrossfadeGamma 1.0, so anti-correlated windows cancel.
- Fix:
  - Search ls, le and xf to maximise the normalised correlation of the two windows (target above 0.9).
  - Write CrossfadeGamma 0.5 (equal power) for decorrelated sources.
  - Or render sustains that are truly periodic, with vibrato, drift and detune beats locked to whole cycles of the loop.
- Loops also replay the same 1-5 s of drift forever, so slow non-periodic wander has to come from the plugin, not only from the sample.

2) NORMALISATION BUG. Verified in code and measured. render_preset sets gain from the RMS of the first 1.0 s.
- Reversed and short sounds get too much gain and are driven into the 0.6 soft-knee limiter. reverse_ghost_chop has 34.6% of samples above the knee, body RMS -6.7 dBFS and a 400 ms level about 16 dB above the bank median. voice_memo_chop, stamp_stutter, hiccup and ah_chop_stack have 2-4% above the knee and peak at -0.5 to -0.9 dBFS.
- Slow-attack vox (ooh_ensemble, angel_stretch_cloud, formant_swirl) play 5-7 dB hot.
- Fix: normalise on the loudest 400 ms (momentary loudness) after all processing, and keep the limiter only as a safety.

3) TOO CLEAN, NO WORLD. Measured.
- 12 of the 20 SIG leads have nothing above 10 kHz (air below -80 dB).
- 7 leads have NTR below -35 dB.
- The 3 legends leads built on gen_sub (cruise_whistle_lead, wet_rim_seraph, ribbon_wraith) are static sines: flux 0.0001 or less, in-sample drift 0.0 c, mono. Their only motion is the plugin's periodic vibrato and a per-note random offset ('Voice Drift' is a Random modulator; one value per note is inferred).
- Bank-wide fix, in four parts:
  1. A source-tied air layer (breath, bow, reed or tape) at -35 to -50 dB under the body, decorrelated L/R.
  2. A sympathetic or halo stage: Ondes Martenot Palme-style 12 chromatic resonators, or a Metallique-style inharmonic gong bank.
  3. Use the existing tape_age (wow, hiss, crackle, bump) on far more recipes.
  4. 1/f pitch and timbre wander instead of sine LFOs.

4) THE ARCHIVE IS 5 RECIPES WITH REAL BUGS. Verified in scripts/generate_library.py and in the audio.
- 16 Hz ticks in all 15 pads: gen_pad calls low_pass_one_pole on each 2756-sample (62.5 ms) chunk, and that function restarts its state at 0 on every call. Energy above 8 kHz at the chunk grid is 29-61 dB above everywhere else, with ticks at about -47 to -56 dBFS under a -15 to -20 dBFS body. Textures, leads, basses and plucks are clean.
- Pad loops end at 6.4 s, inside a baked release that starts at 5.0 s (r = 3.0 s), with only a 45 ms crossfade. Seam steps reach +20.7 dB (solar_drift), +15.3 dB (halo) and +14.3 dB (slow_dawn).
- Leads and plucks fake width with a 1 ms or 0.5 ms delayed copy. C5 squares almost cancel in mono: lab_pinhole and lab_square_vintage have correlation -0.99 and lose 23.1 dB. Saws lose 4-7 dB in mono, crystal_drop 6.5 dB.
- Leads (2.5 s) and basses (3 s) never loop. One 44.1 kHz/16-bit sample covers all 128 keys.
- All 10 plucks are the same recipe at different roots (PLUCK_CONFIGS sets no parameters).
- Near-twins by waveform correlation: storm_eye/vellum 0.9999, pinhole/square_vintage 0.9998, choir_lead/vox_saw 0.9988, halo/slow_dawn 0.968, carve/cellar 0.909. hiss_pad, rain_wall and static sit within 1.4-2.2 dB of each other.
- Text bug: descriptions read '(basse)' because legacy_presets uses f.parent.name.lower()[:-1].
- Macros are templated: BODY 0.25 and ECHO 0.12 on all 50.
- Fix: keep about 14 curated presets, port them into P() recipes (48/24, zones, loops, velocity layers) and retire the rest.

5) VOICE MODEL. Code verified, effect inferred.
- gen_voice uses one fixed formant table for every singer and every preset, including falsetto, angel and helium. 'a' is 730/1090/2440 Hz, which looks like adult-male averages.
- No per-singer formant scatter, 4 c jitter, no shimmer.
- Formants stay fixed when pitch changes, so 'helium' is not varispeed. Ensembles are copies of one vocal tract.
- lcr_ooh_choir and ooh_ensemble have identical recipes.
- Fix:
  - Male, female and child formant tables.
  - Per-singer formant offsets of +-4-6% and choir-range pitch scatter.
  - Shimmer, an LF-style glottal pulse, and a speed above 1 render for varispeed chops.

6) ZONE LOTTERY. Measured.
- Random seeds per zone make neighbouring zones differ in brightness. Worst boundary seams: ah_chop_stack 12.2 st (z60 482 Hz against 690/743 Hz on either side), wood_bass_pluck 10.0, hillside_lament_horn 9.7, breath_vox 9.2, dirty_brass_lead 9.0, angel_stretch_cloud 8.9, halo_smear 8.5.
- Eleven voice-like presets use 6-semitone zones, so formants shift +-3 st at the boundaries.
- Fix: pick seeds against a smooth key-tracked brightness target, or render 2-3 round robins. Use 3 st zones for voice-like sources.

7) VELOCITY LAYERS ARE MOSTLY LEVEL COPIES. Measured.
- In 16 of the 26 two-layer presets here, hard and soft differ in brightness by only x0.98-1.05.
- angel_stretch_cloud is inverted (hard x0.88 of soft) because soften() multiplies voice breath by 1.3.
- Fix: make the soften rules change timbre (lp x0.5-0.6, drive x0.5, softer excitation) and require a ratio of at least 1.25.

8) DUPLICATES. Verified in the recipes and by measurement.
- lcr_ooh_choir = ooh_ensemble
- long_808 ~ long_glide_808 ~ molasses_glide_sub
- grit_808 ~ phone_808
- reese_shadow ~ garage_reese_floor
- brass_sigh_lead ~ dirty_brass_lead
- hyper_saw_lead ~ grit_saw_lead
- voice_memo_chop ~ hiccup
- siren_vox_lead ~ helium_chop
- velvet_square ~ broken_platter_cumbia (spectrum only)
Together with the archive cuts this frees about 40 slots for rare instruments.

9) NOT A MOOG. Verified in code. lp24 is two linear TPT state-variable filters in series, and drive sits outside the loop. A Moog ladder gets its character from tanh in each of its 4 stages and in the feedback (Huovilainen DAFx-04; topology in Moog patent US3475623). Every 'ladder', 'mono' and 'growl' preset inherits the linear filter. Fix: a nonlinear ladder option (2x oversampling, half-sample delay in the feedback path, tuning compensation).

10) STEREO IN THE WRONG PLACE. Measured.
- supersaw pans its voices +-0.9, so mono lines and basses are stereo below 150 Hz: ladder_mono_bass 0.79, lowland_growl_bass 0.74, warehouse_click_organ 0.48.
- Solo leads lose about 2-2.5 dB in mono.
- gen_808, gen_reese, gen_sub and single-singer voice are mono at every frequency, so a reese never widens above its sub.
- Rule, from doc 12: mono below about 120 Hz, width above.

11) SUB LAYER UNDER LEADS. Config verified, effect inferred. BODY drives a sine one octave down (SubOctKnob default -1 per deep_params.json). Leads with BODY 0.2-0.3 and a sub sustain of -8 to -10 dB (dirty_brass_lead, velvet_square, grit_saw_lead, hyper_saw_lead) carry a sine under every melody note.

12) ONSET STEPS. Measured. render_voice subtracts the whole-file mean, so pulse sources start on a step: hillside_lament_horn 0.22 x peak, velvet_square 0.20, boogie_keytar 0.18, broken_platter_cumbia 0.14. Fix: a 5-10 Hz DC-blocking high-pass plus a 1 ms fade-in.

13) 808s ARE MODERN TRAP ONLY. The model has only a fast exponential drop: no attack frequency jump and no slow 'sigh' from the TR-808 circuit (Werner et al.). 3 of the 7 are near copies.

WHAT 'MYSTIC, A WORLD, RARE' MEANS IN RECIPE TERMS. These are proposals, inferred from the sources above:
- Sympathetic strings (Palme) and a metal halo (Metallique).
- Friction tones with harmonics: glass harmonica (Rossing), Cristal Baschet whiskers.
- Overtone chant: one narrow clustered formant (Bloothooft).
- Subharmonic undertones (the Mixtur-Trautonium idea behind the Subharmonicon).
- Brass that brightens with level (Hirschberg).
- Varispeed voices, reverse-reverb ghosts, tape, vinyl and room beds.

'xx synth' is ambiguous. If it means The xx: the Sound On Sound piece on the debut names no synth. It credits sparse parts; Jamie's MPC; Boss DD3 and Roland Space Echo RE301; lead-guitar reverb from a Roland Microcube; a plate on the vocals; and 'the best stuff was the most sparse'. So the recipe is a clean near-sine or triangle lead into a small-amp spring IR plus a tape echo at 1/4D, not a new oscillator. Ask the user which synth he meant.

NEW SLOTS PROPOSED:
- Undertone Lead (replaces grit_saw_lead)
- Bridged-T 808 (long_808)
- Cave Chant (ooh_ensemble)
- Palme Wraith (ribbon_wraith)
- Friction Glass (wet_rim_seraph)
- Talking Shadow (reese_shadow)
- Clay Vessel (hollow_flute)
- Small Amp Echo Lead, xx-style (an archive slot)

None of these needs third-party audio. Any private Keyscape or Omnisphere resampling stays personal-only and never goes in the public repo. Loops (LL_*) were not in this lane.
