### Fact-check (29 items checked by an adversarial verifier)

- **REFUTED**: Pattern 1 headline: 25 of 33 looped presets have crossfade cancellation deeper than -6 dB (tinnitus_siren -30.6, twin_filter_scream -26.8, halo_smear -22.9, angel -20.6, ooh -20.5, hollow_flute -20.0, lcr median -11.9). The dip metric (min 10 ms frame in the simulated fade minus the mean level of the two windows) does not control for amplitude modulation already present in the windows: beats, choir AM, noise, and 10 ms frames on low notes. I re-ran it with a control: crossfade min against the quieter window's own min 10 ms frame (verify_b/seam_ctrl.py and seam_all.py). tinnitus_siren's windows already reach -22.7 dB from its intentional 7 c beat (partials 1.0 and 0.6 at ratio 1.004052), so its median excess is -0.5 dB and its worst -9.7. twin_filter_scream's -26.8 dB frame is at z36, where the untouched windows already reach -27 dB (excess +0.5); its real worst is -8.0 at z90. There are 34 looped presets, not 33. Correction: Excess dip (crossfade min below the quieter window's own min), worst / median per preset: lowland_growl_bass -17.9/-3.7, boogie_keytar -14.5/-3.0, ooh_ensemble -13.4/+0.2, bubble_reed_call -12.7/-4.4, hollow_flute -11.7/-0.1 (z54 corr -0.89), brass_sigh_lead -11.1/-6.0, altiplano -10.9, halo_smear -10.6, siren_vox -10.3, tinnitus -9.7/-0.5, helium -9.1, ladder_mono_bass -8.4, angel -8.3, twin_filter -8.0, lcr_ooh_choir -6.5/-1.9, formant_swirl -3.6/+1.7. 21 of 34 looped presets have at least one zone-channel more than 6 dB below its natural floor; only 9 have a median excess worse than -3 dB. The bug is real (fixed-time loop points, no correlation search) but zone-specific; report these numbers instead of the raw dips.
- **CONFIRMED**: HISE loop crossfade semantics and builder loop points: fade-in [ls-xf, ls) over fade-out [le-xf, le); CrossfadeGamma 1.0 on every map; loop_points uses fixed times and snaps only gen sub/reese. I read HISE 4.1.0 StreamingSamplerSound.cpp (scratchpad copy at tag 4.1.0, rebuildCrossfadeBuffer lines 768-822): fadeInStartOffset = loopStart - xf and fadeOutStartOffset = loopEnd - xf, and the same gamma (clamped 1/32..32) goes to both ramps. ModulatorSamplerSound.cpp sets the gamma before the loop properties at load, so the map value applies. build_signature_bank.py lines 882-891 and 983 confirm ls = max(atk+0.6, 1.0) s, le = n - 30 ms, xf = min(0.35 s, 0.45 x loop, 0.8 x ls), the period snap for sub/reese only, and a hard-coded CrossfadeGamma 1.0. Not verified: the 'local HISE clone develop 6446c4a' is not on disk, so the pow(ramp, gamma) form of hlac applyGainRampWithGamma is unverified here (inferred).
- **UNCERTAIN**: Prescription: write CrossfadeGamma 0.5 (equal power) for decorrelated sources (hollow_flute, altiplano 'on the noisy zones', hyper_saw, lcr, breath_vox, angel, halo). I simulated gamma 0.5 on every looped zone. It raises the median excess by about 1.5-3 dB, but it does not fix anti-correlated windows and sometimes makes them worse: tinnitus worst -9.7 becomes -14.7, lowland -17.9 becomes -21.0, and hollow_flute's worst zone (corr -0.89) is still -9.0. CrossfadeGamma is an attribute of the samplemap root, so 'only on the noisy zones' cannot be expressed. write_preset_audio hard-codes 1.0, so a hand edit to SIG_*.xml is lost on the next build. On correlated (periodic) material, equal power adds a +3 dB bump at mid-fade. Correction: Primary fix: search ls/le/xf for maximum window correlation. Use gamma 0.5 only on whole maps that are noise-dominated (breath_vox, altiplano_breath), added as a new P()/JSON field that the builder writes, never as a hand edit to the XML. Never use it on period-snapped sub/reese maps.
- **CONFIRMED**: Pattern 2: render_preset normalises on the first 1.0 s RMS; reverse_ghost_chop 34.6% over the 0.6 knee and about 16 dB above the bank median; slow-attack vox 5-7 dB hot. Code lines 902-918: per-zone gain and bank norm both use slice(0, 1.0 s). Measured SIG_reverse_ghost_chop_60_v2 (1.6 s file): first 1 s from file start -18.5 dBFS, max 400 ms -1.3 dBFS, peak -0.4 dBFS. The other numbers are slightly off: the whole-file share over 0.6 is 26.3%, not 34.6% (the share depends on the window). The 'vs median -17.9' comparisons mix metrics: -17.9 is the K-proxy median, the -1.3/-11.0/-11.7 figures are unweighted, and the unweighted non-archive median is -16.75.
- **CONFIRMED**: Pattern 4: archive pads tick at 16 Hz from low_pass_one_pole resetting state per 62.5 ms chunk; textures clean. generate_library.py lines 94-106 (prev = 0.0 on every call) and 205-212 (chunk = 44100//16 = 2756). Measured HF energy above 8 kHz on the chunk grid against off-grid: halo +61.1 dB, solar_drift +36.4 dB, marrow +67.6 dB (verify_b/spot.py). Textures use the same reset bug with 125 ms chunks (lines 313-338), but the noise masks it: +0.1 to +0.6 dB on the grid for hiss_pad, furnace_drone and wind_tunnel.
- **CONFIRMED**: Archive pad loop ends at 6.4 s inside the release that starts at 5.0 s, xf 45 ms; seam steps +20.7 (solar_drift), +15.3 (halo), +14.3 (slow_dawn). The map XML has SampleEnd 352800 (8 s at 44.1k), LoopStart 70560 (1.6 s), LoopEnd 282240 (6.4 s), LoopXFade 2000 (45 ms). envelope(a=1.2, d=0.8, s=0.78, r=3.0) puts the release start at 5.0 s. My window choice measured steps of +18.7/+19.0 dB (solar_drift), +10.8/+12.3 (halo) and +10.6/+12.0 (slow_dawn), with the window levels 7-15 dB apart.
- **CONFIRMED**: Archive leads/plucks: R = L delayed 1 ms / 0.5 ms; lab_pinhole and lab_square_vintage corr -0.99, mono fold -23.1 dB; crystal_drop -6.5 dB; leads/basses never loop; one 44.1k sample for all 128 keys. Code lines 253-255 and 300-302 (int(0.001*44100) = 44 samples, about 0.52 of a C5 period). Measured exactly: pinhole and square_vintage corr -0.99, mono -23.1 dB; crystal_drop -0.55 / -6.5 dB; cutter -0.34 / -4.8 dB. The maps have LoKey 0, HiKey 127, and LoopEnabled 0 for leads and basses. The two squares are not byte-identical (cutoff 5500 vs 4500, same root) but are near-identical.
- **CONFIRMED**: Archive text bug '(basse)' and templated macros BODY 0.25 / ECHO 0.12 on all 50. legacy_presets line 1064 uses f.parent.name.lower()[:-1], so 'Basses' becomes 'basse'; the other folders are fine. Line 1060 hard-codes m[1] = 0.25 and m[5] = 0.12.
- **CONFIRMED**: Pattern 9: lp24 is two linear TPT SVFs with drive outside the loop, so no Moog character; 'use the per-voice filter ladder' as an alternative. sigbank_dsp.py lp24 (lines 162-165) is svf(svf()) with no nonlinearity, and drive_pre/drive are tanh before and after the filter. Extension: the plugin's per-voice FTypeKnob options (deep_params.json filter_types) map 'LADDER LP' to HISE mode 15 (LadderFourPoleLP, LadderSubType) and 'WARM LP' to mode 8 (MoogLP, MoogFilterSubType). In HISE 4.1.0 MultiChannelFilters.cpp (LadderSubType::processSample lines 904-916; MoogFilterSubType::processSamples 388-411), both are linear: feedback with no tanh. Werner/Huovilainen source details were not re-read by me.
- **CONFIRMED**: Pattern 10: supersaw pans voices +-0.9, making 'mono' basses stereo below 150 Hz (ladder_mono 0.79, lowland 0.74); gen_808/reese/sub/single-singer voice fully mono. gen_supersaw line 426 pn = u * spread (default 0.9) per voice, with independent drift per voice. gen_reese and gen_808 return x, x.copy(); gen_sub returns identical channels. The CSV low_corr values match.
- **REFUTED**: warehouse_click_organ: 'beat_c decorrelates the sub'; prescription: apply beat_c only above 150 Hz. gen_modal (lines 515-519) gives each side of every mode its own random phase rng.uniform(0, 6.28). beat_c 0.3 c is a +-0.3 c L/R detune: at the 0.5 mode on z36 (32.7 Hz) it drifts only about 7 degrees over the 1.6 s sample. The low-end correlation of 0.48 comes from the random per-side phase, not from beat_c. Correction: Use one shared phase for L and R on modes below about 150 Hz; beat_c can stay. Level fix (trim 8 down) stands, but the offset is +4.8 dB (K-proxy) to +7.6 dB (unweighted) above the median, not 'about 9 dB'.
- **REFUTED**: Pattern 8 / grit_saw_lead: 'Duplicates hyper_saw_lead; its only difference is drive'. Recipes: grit_saw voices 3, detune 10 c, lp 4000, drive 2.5; hyper_saw voices 5, detune 16 c, lp 7000, no drive, PUMP 0.30. The 1/3-octave distance is 2.85 dB and the envelope distance 2.77 dB, just under the dupe thresholds. Correction: They are 'spectrally close'. Unison width and cutoff also differ, so GRIT on hyper_saw_lead does not reproduce grit_saw_lead. Retiring it is still a defensible judgement call.
- **CONFIRMED**: Pattern 8: lcr_ooh_choir = ooh_ensemble; long_808 ~ long_glide_808 ~ molasses; grit_808 ~ phone_808; reese_shadow ~ garage_reese_floor. lcr_ooh_choir and ooh_ensemble have identical gp (singers 6, u, vib_c 14, breath 0.05, attack 0.35), z (48, 84, 6) and dur 4.0; only the seeds and macro/voicing values differ (BODY 0 vs 0.25, subsus -30 vs -12, atk 400 vs 500). The 808 and reese pairs differ by small parameter offsets in the recipes; partb_dupes.csv distances match.
- **REFUTED**: Pattern 12 onset DC steps: velvet_square 0.20, hillside 0.22, boogie 0.18, broken_platter 0.14 x peak, labelled as z60. These are worst-over-zones values, not z60. At z60 (the file the metric strings cite): velvet_square 0.05, hillside_lament_horn 0.13 (v2), boogie_keytar 0.17, broken_platter_cumbia 0.01. The worst zones are velvet z48 0.20, hillside z72 v1 0.22, boogie z54 v1 0.18, broken z84 v1 0.14 (verify_b/onset.py). The cause is confirmed: render_voice line 865 subtracts the whole-file mean, and saw-only fat_mono_line shows 0.00, which fits pulse-wave DC. Correction: Relabel these as worst-zone values. Impact is small, because the plugin amp attack (1-60 ms) ramps the offset in. DC-block plus a 1 ms fade remains a cheap fix.
- **REFUTED**: Pattern 7: 16 of 26 two-layer presets are level copies (x0.98-1.05); angel_stretch_cloud inverted x0.88 'because soften() multiplies voice breath by 1.3'. The count from the lane's own CSV is 15, not 16. Cause test (verify_b/veltest.py): I rendered gen_voice with angel's hard gp and its soften() gp on the same seed at z61/70/73. The centroid ratio is 1.00 with the breath x1.3 and 1.00 without it, so neither the breath change nor lp x0.75 moves brightness: the voice energy sits below 1.5 kHz. With different seeds (as the builder uses: seed ^ 0x50F7), the hard and soft centroids each spread about 500-550 Hz, so the x0.88 inversion is seed lottery. Correction: For voice presets, 'soft layer lp x0.6' will also do nothing. Velocity timbre needs a source change (glottal tilt mix, formant gain, singer count) and the same seed for both layers. The 1.25 ratio target is a proposal, not a measured norm.
- **UNCERTAIN**: Pattern 6 zone lottery: random seeds per zone cause neighbouring-zone brightness jumps (ah_chop 12.2 st, wood_bass 10.0, hillside 9.7, breath_vox 9.2, angel 8.9). Seeds matter: rendering ah_chop_stack's gp at one root with 6 seeds spreads the centroid 3.3-5.0 st (verify_b/seedtest.py). But the seam metric resamples each zone half a step to the boundary, and for fixed-formant voices that alone gives a seam of about one zone step. With median seeds, the 6-st zones already show 2.9-7.1 st seams. Roughly half of ah_chop's 12.2 st is structural (6-st zones), half lottery. wood_bass_pluck's spread fits a random one-period noise burst (gen_karplus lines 476-483). Correction: Present it as two causes: 6-st zones on formant sources (fix: 3-st zones), plus per-zone seeds (fix: seed selection or round robins). For voice-like presets the seam metric overstates the lottery share.
- **REFUTED**: Pattern 3: cruise_whistle_lead is a static sine with 'nothing above the 2nd harmonic'; ribbon_wraith a near-sine; wet_rim_seraph a pure sine. Harmonics measured at 0.5-1.5 s (verify_b/harm.py). cruise_whistle_lead z60 v2: H2 -14.1, H3 -21.3, H4 -22.9, H5 -32.1 dB, from tanh drive 1.4. ribbon_wraith: H3 -18.3 dB (drive 1.5). wet_rim_seraph: H3 -33 dB, effectively a sine (confirmed). Static and mono are confirmed (gen_sub has no drift; L = R). Correction: cruise_whistle_lead has a soft-clipped harmonic series to about H5. Stronger version of 'only plugin vibrato': cruise_whistle_lead and ribbon_wraith set ViaWheelKnob 1, so vibrato needs the mod wheel; at wheel 0 the only motion is the per-note Random 'Voice Drift' (verified as a HISE Random processor, ResearchFacility.xml line 239) plus plugin chorus/reverb.
- **UNCERTAIN**: Pattern 13 / Werner DAFx-14: 808 model has 'no attack frequency jump and no slow sigh'. Paper text (scratchpad dafx.txt lines 31, 89, 114-121) confirms that the envelope generator alters the bridged-T centre frequency for the first few ms, that leakage through the retriggering pulse causes the pitch 'sigh', and that the paper distinguishes the sigh from the attack frequency jump. But gen_808 (lines 646-665) does have an attack pitch jump: an exponential drop of drop_st (3-24 st) with tau 20-50 ms. Correction: Say: gen_808 has one fast exponential attack drop (a modern trap-808 contour) and no slow sigh across the decay. Adding sigh_c is the valid part.
- **REFUTED**: Prescriptions using 'd: Glide' (boogie_keytar 40 ms, fat_mono_line 60 ms, cruise_whistle_lead 80 ms). deep_params.json has no Glide control. check_deep (lines 1076-1083) asserts 'unknown SHAPE control', so the build fails. Interface.js has no glide or portamento code; docs/13 lists glide via Synth.addPitchFade only as a plan. Correction: Glide needs a plugin feature first (Legato with Retrigger plus Synth.addPitchFade, per docs/13) and a new deep_params entry. Until then, drop the Glide lines from the recipes.
- **REFUTED**: Per-layer prescriptions ('post_shelf on v2 only', 'soft layer lp x0.6', 'hard layer drive_pre 4.0', 'fenv x0.5 on the soft layer', 'drive x0.6 on the soft layer'). The recipe format has no per-preset soft-layer override. The soft layer is soften(gen, gp), driven by the per-generator SOFT_RULES table (lines 797-824), and the hard layer is gp as written. load_instrument_packs rejects unknown top-level JSON fields (RECIPE_KEYS assert). For supersaw, SOFT_RULES already scales lp x0.65 and drive_pre x0.7. Correction: Either add a new P()/JSON field (for example 'soft': {param: multiplier}) and extend RECIPE_KEYS, or change SOFT_RULES, which changes every preset on that generator.
- **CONFIRMED**: Recipe-format risk: the many 'new gp' keys (pwm, scoop_c, shimmer_db, bits, formants, lr_detune, am, mid_spread, grain, speed, sigh_c, tail_drive, growl_hz, h3, subs, drift_c on gen_sub). gp is a free dict with no per-generator key validation. A key added to a recipe before it is implemented in sigbank_dsp.py builds cleanly and changes nothing.
- **UNCERTAIN**: Pattern 3 fix parts 1 and 3: add air, and apply tape_age to far more recipes (for example velvet_square tape {wow_c 2, hiss 0.001}, cruise tape without lp). tape_age defaults (lines 753-787) apply when keys are omitted: lp24 at 7000 Hz, flutter 3 c at 9 Hz, bump 0.25, sat 1.2 and hiss 0.004. The hiss is noise low-passed at 6 kHz. supersaw 'noise' is also low-passed at 4 kHz. Tape as specified would remove air above 7 kHz, and neither noise source adds energy above 10 kHz. Correction: When air is the goal, set tape lp explicitly (12-16 kHz) and add a high-passed air-noise option. Otherwise the tape fix works against the air fix.
- **UNCERTAIN**: tinnitus_siren prescription: second partial ratio 1 + k/(f0 x loop_len) for 'about a 2 Hz beat' per zone. The maths is right: integer beats plus a whole-period loop make both partials periodic. But the recipe's fixed 7.0 c ratio (1.004052) makes the beat rise with pitch (about 2.1 Hz at C5, about 8.5 Hz at C7). A fixed ~2 Hz beat everywhere changes the design. The loop-dip problem behind this prescription is mostly natural beat nulls (excess median -0.5 dB). Correction: Keep the 7 c character: k = round(0.004052 x f0 x L), and snap L to whole f0 periods. Turn off the glass_pad L/R detune and AM as proposed.
- **REFUTED**: lowland_growl_bass prescription: 'with spread 0 and locked drift it is periodic, so snap the loop to whole periods'. The recipe keeps voices 3 at detune_c 3.5 (offsets -3.5, 0, +3.5 c). The resulting beat at 65 Hz has a period of about 7.6 s, so the signal is not periodic over a 2 s loop even with spread 0 and drift 0. Its seam cancellation is real (z27 excess -17.9 dB). Correction: Either detune 0 plus a whole-period snap, or lock the detune beat to the loop length (as for tinnitus). Spread 0 for the low end stands.
- **REFUTED**: wood_bass_pluck prescription: add a wooden body modal bank (2-3 modes 90-220 Hz). gen_karplus already has 'body' (lines 489-491): band-pass resonances at 110 Hz and 210 Hz, and the recipe sets body 0.8. Correction: Tune or extend the existing body option rather than adding a duplicate stage. The deterministic-excitation fix for zone lottery stands.
- **UNCERTAIN**: paredao_pressure 'previews about 5 dB weaker'; warehouse_click_organ 'about 9 dB over median'. The figures mix the unweighted max-400 value (trim included) with the K-proxy median. Like-for-like: paredao is -6.4 dB (unweighted, vs -16.75) or -6.6 dB (K-proxy, vs -17.9); warehouse is +7.6 or +4.8. Why the -8.5 trim was chosen is not documented. Correction: paredao: about -6.5 dB, so trim about -2 to -3, re-checked after any normalisation change. warehouse: +5 to +8 dB, so trim about 2-3.
- **CONFIRMED**: formant_swirl: a-o-e-u morph spans the whole 4 s file, so every loop cycle snaps back. _formant_filter interpolates the vowels at linspace(0, n-1, nv), so a, o, e and u sit at 0, 1.33, 2.67 and 4.0 s. The loop runs 1.0 s (mostly 'o') to 3.97 s ('u'). Its crossfade excess is small (worst -3.6 dB), so the defect is the vowel/level jump, not cancellation.
- **CONFIRMED**: Voice model: one fixed formant table ('a' 730/1090/2440) for every singer and preset; helium has fixed formants; ensembles share one tract. FORMANTS dict (lines 561-568) and gen_voice (lines 594-629): every singer uses the same table, jitter is a 2-sine 4 c drift at 1.5-3 Hz, and there is no shimmer. render_voice supports only slow < 1, not speed > 1. The values match the widely reproduced Peterson-Barney male averages (my inference; the table was not read).
- **UNCERTAIN**: Web sources not re-checked by this verifier (SOS 'Recording The xx' gear list, Huovilainen DAFx-04 details, Rossing JASA 1994, Ondes Palme/Metallique, Baschet, Bloothooft, Ternstrom, Prame). No saved copies are in the scratchpad except the Werner DAFx-14 text, which I did check. The lane already marks most of these as excerpt-only. I made no web fetches. Correction: Treat the xx gear list (MPC, DD3, RE-301, Microcube, plate) as unverified until the SOS page is read in full, and ask the user what 'xx synth' means before building it.

## 5. Loop kit audit (64 kits)

### Method

Read-only audit of all 64 Loop Lines kits: the 3 built-ins in scripts/build_loop_lines.py KITS, using the template pattern(), plus 61 assets/packs/loop_kits/*.json. They are rendered as LL_<kit>_<lane><take>.wav. I compared the 1,024 installed takes byte for byte with hise_project/ResearchFacility/Samples: all identical. Python 3.12, numpy and scipy only, no installs, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1.

Scripts are in /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-noxvitae/0e6ab598-c428-4549-a613-b529408ad567/scratchpad/wf_mystic/loops/:

- dump_kits.py: imports build_loop_lines and build_signature_bank read-only. Dumps every kit's events through kit_events() with lane preset gen/gp/m/v into kits.json.
- pattern_metrics.py (pat.json), symbolic, per take:
  - onsets/beat;
  - anticipation-synco: share of 8th/16th off-beat onsets whose next quarter has no onset in the same lane (my simplified measure);
  - register, pitch classes, chord-tone share, bar repetition;
  - take Jaccard: overlap of onset cells between takes of a lane;
  - per kit: cross-lane rub (share of shared 16th steps with a 1 or 13 semitone interval between lanes), onset overlap, median MIDI;
  - long-note truncation risk against render_loop's 6 s voice cap and 2-loop fold buffer (0 hits).
- harm.py (harm.json): KEYS chord changes per loop (pitch-class-set changes per half beat).
- audio_metrics.py (audio.json):
  - RMS, peak, crest;
  - ungated BS.1770 K-weighted loudness using the 48 kHz coefficients (https://www.itu.int/rec/R-REC-BS.1770);
  - seam d1 jump vs local d1 RMS;
  - spectral centroid, flatness, energy share >8 kHz and <120 Hz;
  - quiet floor = 5th percentile of 20 ms frame RMS;
  - L/R correlation and side/mid ratio;
  - band motion = mean over 32 log bands of the dB standard deviation over time;
  - spectral-flux onset lag after each pattern onset.
- click_metrics.py (clicks.json): onset step = the largest single-sample jump in the first 4 samples after each note start.
- overs.py (overs.json): soft-knee pressure = share of samples with |y| >= 0.9. That means the pre-knee value passed about +1.1 dBFS in soft_knee().
- kit_digest.py and metrics_str.py build the per-kit lines. In those lines, K/B/P/A = KEYS/BASS/PLUCK/AIR.

Caveats:
- A stray-click detector (second difference and high-pass bursts) was dropped as unreliable. It fired on the periodic glottal pulses of the voice generator and on saw edges, not on defects.
- Seam check: all 76 takes with a sizeable jump at the loop seam have a note starting on beat 1. The fold itself is clean.
- Code facts were verified by reading the files:
  - render_loop, soft_knee and the cache seed in build_loop_lines.py;
  - render_voice in build_signature_bank.py;
  - gen_808 in sigbank_dsp.py;
  - applyLoopFx and applyFeel in Interface.js;
  - KeyShift.js and SampleRouter.js;
  - the Loop Lines / Loop Vel / Loop Env nodes in XmlPresetBackups/ResearchFacility.xml.
- External claims:
  - Fetched and verified this session: mixonline (Josh Gudwin), Sound On Sound (Frank Dukes p2, 40 'Headlines'), and Wikipedia (Balinese gamelan, Compás, Huayno).
  - Taken from the repo's own fact-checked docs/12, docs/12b and docs/13, with the URLs those docs give, not re-fetched by me: playlist tempos and keys, LATIN MAFIA, rusowsky, Ralphie Choo, Milo J, Tainy.
  - The session's web-search budget ran out, so no further searches were made.

### Bank-wide patterns

BANK-WIDE PROBLEMS. All verified in code or in the rendered audio unless marked otherwise.

1. LOOPS ARE DRY GENERATOR RENDERS
- build_loop_lines.render_loop calls render_voice with the raw gp.
- Nothing from the preset's voicing is baked: v.atk/v.rel, TONE, SPACE, ECHO, WIDTH and GRIT are all left out.
- Release is capped at 0.25 s: rel = min(0.25, 0.6*hold + 0.05).
- Interface.js applyLoopFx then puts every kit through the same state: filter open, reverb wet 0.08, echo 0, drive 0, chorus width 0, pump 0.
- So all 64 kits share one room. Affected lanes out of 256:
  - 65 rely on SPACE >= 0.6 or ECHO >= 0.3 (e.g. dotted_echo_arp at ECHO 0.75, frozen_halo at SPACE 0.85);
  - 63 have preset release >= 1 s;
  - 23 have a TONE darker than their baked gp.lp.
- Supporting source (verified): Josh Gudwin says 'if a synth has a big reverb, and it's timed out right and it's part of the sound, that stays in' (https://www.mixonline.com/recording/josh-gudwin-on-mixing-part-1-juanes-bad-bunny-and-dua-lipa).
- Fix:
  - render each lane through its own voicing: LP at m[0], envelope from v.atk/v.rel, an offline reverb/echo scaled by m[4]/m[5] at the kit tempo, drive from m[6];
  - fold the tails into the loop;
  - optionally print a dry/wet pair, as Gudwin also describes.

2. MONO
- 49 of 192 non-bass lanes are bit-identical L=R: every tine_ep, sub and reed_ep lane, 12/32 karplus lanes and 9/29 voice lanes.
- 7 lanes have negative L/R correlation; worst is ventana_abril PLUCK (-0.33, side 2.9 dB over mid).
- LOOPS mode also zeroes chorus width, so nothing restores stereo at playback.

3. NO NOISE FLOOR
- 24 of 256 lane medians fall to digital silence (5th-percentile frame under -90 dBFS); 60 fall under -60 dBFS.
- Frank Dukes' sampler deliberately keeps analog synths' 'baseline noise ... not getting rid of it' (https://www.soundonsound.com/people/frank-dukes?page=2, verified).

4. LEVEL BALANCE
- Every take is normalised to the same RMS (about -13.2 dBFS). In K-weighted loudness:
  - BASS sits about 2.5 dB under KEYS (median);
  - AIR pads are as loud as KEYS (+0.3 dB);
  - FM bells run up to 3.4 dB over.
- Playback leaves almost no room to rebalance: no per-lane mixer exists, Loop Vel intensity is 0.35 (about 3.7 dB range), and Mac keys always send velocity 127.
- The soft knee works hard:
  - 431 of 1,024 takes contain samples whose pre-knee value passed full scale;
  - 241 exceed 1 permille, worst 23.6 permille, all in PLUCK lanes;
  - puna_rasgueo pluck4 sits flat at the 0.95 ceiling.
- Fix: render to per-lane loudness targets, e.g. KEYS 0, BASS +2, PLUCK -2, AIR -4 dB relative (inferred starting values, set by ear). Cap the make-up gain at the knee, and render 24-bit as docs/15 planned.

5. ONSET CLICKS
- Notes get no attack ramp in render_loop.
- 169 takes have a single-sample onset step above -20 dBFS. Worst presets: lg_highland_ten +0.3, perreo_keys -1.4, funk_pick_clav -2.2, chorus_jangle_clean -5.3, porch_nylon -5.3 dBFS.
- perreo_keys already starts with a step in its SIG samples.
- The loop seam fold is clean: there are no truncations, and all 76 seam jumps are beat-1 attacks.
- Fix: a 1-3 ms raised-cosine ramp per note, or the preset's v.atk.

6. MACHINE-GUN REPEATS AND FLAT VELOCITY
- 39% of all note events replay a sample-identical voice. The cache is keyed on (note, length) and the seed on preset + note, so the same note is identical across takes and kits.
- Velocity changes gain only, as (vel/127)^1.4; soften() is never applied, so accents do not change brightness.
- Fix: seed per event; above a velocity threshold blend the soften() layer; add per-note micro-detune and a start-offset jitter.

7. LATE SWELLS
- About 30 lanes have a generator attack of 35% or more of the note length. Examples: sunroof AIR 1.10, saudade AIR 1.00, halation AIR 0.97, fern AIR 0.82, ribbon KEYS 0.78.
- Notes start on the beat, so pads peak late.
- Fix: let pattern events pre-roll by the attack time. The fold already wraps negative starts. Allow start < 0 in load_kit_packs.

8. NO GLIDE
- gen_808 re-drops its pitch on every note, and render_loop has no legato.
- So 'glide' and 'slide' presets (long_glide_808, molasses_glide_sub, cold_slide) and leads (whistle, ribbon, siren vox) cannot portamento.
- Fix: add an optional glide target per event and render it as a continuous voice.

9. FORM AND RANGE
- 59 of 64 kits are 2 bars with one progression.
- The builder asserts bars in (2, 4) at 4 beats per bar, so no 3/4 or 6/8 meter exists. 12/8 is faked with triplets inside 4/4.
- There is no Song Key or root row: kits play only in their rendered key.
- The docs/15 Flip and Halftime shapers and the 3 RR groups were not built.
- wood_bass_pluck is the BASS in 14 of 64 kits (17 distinct basses in total).
- The 3 built-in kits share one identical rhythm grid.

10. PLAYBACK SYNC (playback bug, not content)
- Loop Lines lanes are not quantized. SampleRouter delays only channel 15 (the SAMPLES page), and no LoopRouter exists (grep of the XML and scripts).
- A lane pressed late stays offset from the others for as long as it plays. HISE has no phase lock (docs/15, source-read at the 4.1.0 tag).
- Fix: route lane notes through the same grid/delayEvent logic with Snap = loop length.

MISSING PLAYLIST VIBES
Tempos and keys are from docs/12 and docs/12b, which cite their own sources.

- Emotive club 134-138 in the Fred again.. x LATIN MAFIA mould:
  - Bonita, 134, A major (https://songbpm.com/@fred-again/bonita-aav1k);
  - Alvafro, 138, F minor, half-time 69 intro (https://songbpm.com/@latin-mafia/alvafro-x3jbc);
  - shape: voice-note fragments, plus found sounds (LATIN MAFIA keep speaker static, a steel drum, soap; docs/13, https://es.rollingstone.com/todos-los-dias-todo-el-dia-sintiendo-con-latin-mafia/).
- R&B-infused Afrobeats at 96 in C# minor (Flores; Spin descriptor, https://songbpm.com/@latin-mafia/flores-tv6w7). fern_shrine_steel has the tresillo but not the R&B.
- Melodic and OVO-style house at 122-126 in A minor and Eb/F# minor: Bleu nuage J'attends que la nuit (124) and Que la vie est belle (126); Flight's Booked and Chicago Freestyle (122-123). Only pulse_house and last_train_glow sit near this pocket.
- Future beats / wonky, about 110, C minor: Flume's re-work of Seekae, 'jittery, shimmering' (https://ra.co/reviews/15613 per docs/12b).
- Indietronica and synth-pop:
  - The Look: A minor i-iv-VI, 85/170;
  - Borderline: 98, harmonic-minor V7;
  - The Spins: 127;
  - Sweet Disposition: 129 D major, Dadd11-Bm(add11) intro (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sweet_Disposition).
- Folk singer-songwriter at 95 in B major, IVmaj7-vi-I (Sailor Song, https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sailor_Song_(song)). No acoustic folk-ballad kit exists.
- Fast bedroom surf in A or B major at 157-184 (Night Walks 157, Blade Running 163, Freaks 180, Televisions 184). Only saltwater_basement, in D, covers it.
- Strokes-style garage:
  - Someday: A-Bm9-Dmaj7;
  - Under Cover of Darkness: vi7-IV-I-Vsus4;
  - Call It Fate: Gadd9-Cmaj7-Am(add9)-D+ with an old-radio colour (https://www.hooktheory.com/theorytab/view/the-strokes/call-it-fate-call-it-karma).
- Spanish experimental alt-pop (rusowsky / Ralphie Choo): voice as instrument, iPhone-recorded straw and water-bottle flute, two sounds far apart (https://www.thefader.com/2023/10/23/ralphie-choo-supernova-gen-f-interview-2023).
- Triple-meter emo / cloud: BONES Cotton, 114 in 3/4, E major (single source). This needs 3/4 builder support.
- Psych-soul lounge and 80s bedroom synth-funk:
  - Sorry Bro: Fmaj7-Cmaj7-E7-Am7;
  - Something About You: F-Em-Am-Dm;
  - Sink into the Floor: 79, Db.

WAYS TO ADD MYSTERY, WORLD AND FEELING (per-kit levers)
- Bake each lane's own space and echo (item 1).
- Add a fifth 'WORLD' bed lane, or a per-kit bed: room tone, found sound, hiss or crackle that never drops to silence (Dukes; LATIN MAFIA).
- Render detuned pairs for metal and bell kits. Paired instruments 'tuned slightly apart to produce interference beats' are the Balinese ombak (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balinese_gamelan, verified).
- Add per-kit tuning tables in cents for the pelog, Andean and flamenco kits (inferred).
- Use sample-rate degradation for the OVO kits. 40 pulled the 'Headlines' bass to 4400 Hz in Lo-Fi (https://www.soundonsound.com/techniques/noah-40-shebib-recording-drakes-headlines, verified).
- Pan lanes far apart (rusowsky, docs/13).
- Add pre-rolled and reversed swells and tape-start pitch scoops.
- Use 4-bar forms with bar-4 turnarounds.
- Write genre-true accent cycles:
  - compás accents on 3, 6, 8, 10, 12 (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Compás, verified);
  - huayno: a stressed beat followed by two short beats (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Huayno, verified).
- Seed per event, and blend soften() by velocity, so loops breathe instead of looping identical samples.
- All third-party material stays out. The loops are fully procedural (meta license 'research-facility-original'), so no personal-only flags apply here.

| velvet_undertow | texture | 62 BPM 2 bars / onsets/beat 1.53/0.94/1.22/0.34 / anticip-synco 0.31/0.12/0.34/0.00 / KEYS chord changes/loop [2,1,5,1] / worst rub 7% / median MIDI 66/36/82/72 / LUFS -11.1/-13.1/-10.4/-10.4 / floor -64/-54/-75/-18 dBFS / >8k -74/-51/-65/-26 dB / motion 13.6/15.8/17.0/3.3 dB / mono: KEYS / knee overs 10.5 permille (P) / identical-voice repeats 13% | The slowed ambient trap concept depends on space. But three lanes are built on SPACE 0.75, which LOOPS removes, half_speed_keys is mono and dark, and static_bloom is frozen (3.3 dB motion). | Bake SPACE/ECHO into the renders. Make the KEYS stereo. Add a slow filter LFO to the AIR, plus a reversed-swell AIR take. |

The charango KEYS is the loudest lane (-8.6 LUFS), with a -2 dBFS onset step and 13 permille overs.
