### Fact-check (23 items checked by an adversarial verifier)

- **CONFIRMED**: Method: installed LL_ takes byte-identical to hise_project Samples; knee-overs threshold |y|>=0.9 equals pre-knee 1.137 (+1.1 dBFS). Verified: md5 matched for 20 spot-checked files. Recomputed soft_knee (knee 0.5, ceil 0.95): y>=0.9 needs a >= 0.5+0.45*atanh(0.889) = 1.137. Top permille values reproduce: honeycomb_step pluck4 23.6, corduroy_pursuit pluck4 22.8, neon_perreo bass4 21.3. Long-note truncation check also reproduces 0 hits; the longest note is 4.2 s (omen_bells AIR take 4).
- **CONFIRMED**: LOOPS mode FX state (TONE open, reverb 8%, ECHO/GRIT/chorus width 0). Verified in Interface.js applyLoopFx (lines 3785-3797): FX_Filter fFreq 20000, rWet 0.08, eMix 0, dSat/dWet 0, cWid 0, pump and M_Move off. It applies to all lanes at once as master FX, so per-lane character can only come from the render.
- **REFUTED**: night_drive: 'the render bakes neither' TONE nor SPACE; prescription 'bake TONE 2200 Hz, decim 11025 and SPACE 0.6'. Verified in build_signature_bank.render_voice and gen_tine_ep. The render already bakes gp lp 2200 (keytracked) plus decim 11025 for drowned_tines, and lp 1500 plus decim 10000 for submerged_choir. KEYS take 1 has a measured centroid of about 300 Hz, so the 'drowned' tone is there. What is missing is SPACE, ECHO and WIDTH, and the plugin-side TONE filter stage. Correction: Bake only reverb, echo and width (and optionally the extra TONE stage). Re-applying decim 11025 would double the sample-and-hold plus svf smoothing.
- **REFUTED**: night_drive rework: bars 4, move to assets/packs/loop_kits/night_drive.json and keep it at index 0 (also eight_second_vespers bars 4, black_glove_lullaby optional 4-bar). Verified from code. KIT_MB_CAP is 32 MB, and 16 takes x 16 beats x 60/bpm x 48000 x 4 bytes means 4 bars fits only at about 93 BPM or faster. Sizes at 4 bars: night_drive (72 BPM) 41.0 MB, eight_second_vespers (76) 38.8 MB, black_glove_lullaby (88) 33.5 MB, so build_loop_lines main() asserts and stops. Packs load after KITS in filename order, so a pack file cannot sit at index 0 without a loader change. Index 0 matters: KitKey's default text, the fresh-instance load(0) and the selftest at Interface.js:4107 (quarters == 8, confirmed) all depend on it. Correction: Keep night_drive in the built-in KITS dict and add 'patterns' (and prog_bars) there; render_loop and kit_events already read them. Stay at 2 bars, or raise KIT_MB_CAP. 4 bars is safe for pulse_house at 124 (23.8 MB) and rede_entardecer at 120 (24.6 MB).
- **CONFIRMED**: neon_perreo retire: duplicate of neon_perreo_ii, findKit alias needed. Verified. Same bpm 98, root 54 (F# minor) and i-iv shape. BASS/PLUCK/AIR are identical (dembow_bass, dembow_nylon, neon_haze); only KEYS differs. findKit returns -1 and onKitKey then does nothing, so a saved set keeps whatever kit is loaded. Removing index 1 does not break the selftest, which only checks findKit(LOOP_KITS[1].id).
- **CONFIRMED**: perreo_keys onset click (-1 dBFS step, SIG_perreo_keys starts at -0.15); fix = fade-in after DC removal in render_voice. Verified. Rendered voice x[0] is 0.195 of peak. SIG_perreo_keys_*.wav first sample is about -0.15 FS. In the loop the step is -1.4 dBFS at every onset and is the largest step in the file. Root cause: sigbank_dsp.py:309 'x = x - np.mean(x[:2048])' in gen_tine_ep subtracts a constant from the zero-amplitude start; render_voice's global mean subtraction does the same for other gens. It is wider than perreo_keys: lg_bark_stage (x0 0.10 of peak) makes neon_perreo_ii KEYS take 2 step -11 dBFS against a p99 of -22, and also sunroof_bounce and lotus KEYS. velvet_square (0.148, luar_de_baile PLUCK) and grit_808 (0.09: cold_slide, halo_mosh, carousel BASS) are affected too.
- **REFUTED**: Karplus / fm_bell / supersaw-pluck onset steps read as clicks (chorus_jangle_clean -5, porch_nylon -5, lg_highland_ten +0.3, clav -2, crystal_fm_bell -8, cloud_fm_bell -6, lg_barrio_pluck -5, five_take_steel -7 dBFS) and the 'add 1.5 ms attack ramp' / '2 ms ramp' prescriptions. Verified. gen_karplus already applies a 1.5 ms ramp (sigbank_dsp.py:493). Rendered voices start at about 0, with onset steps 32-67 dB below peak (karplus), about 20-24 dB below (fm_bell) and about 22 dB below (lg_barrio_pluck), while later sample-to-sample steps reach 0.4-1.05 x peak. In the loops the 'onset step' is ordinary HF motion, not a click. lowrider_sunset pluck1: onset -8 dBFS, random-window p99 -2.9, global max +4.8 dBFS. cold_slide pluck1: onset median -18.1, random median -12.4. The click_metrics measure is only valid for smooth sources (tine_ep, 808, reese). Correction: Drop the ramp prescriptions for bedroom_interlock, desvelo_serrano, sierra_humo, puna_rasgueo, saudade_quarter_inch, the corduroy/fire_escape/lowrider clav, sotano_brasa, cold_slide and sugar_glass bells; they would only dull pick attacks. Keep the brightness and level findings.
- **REFUTED**: Lane trims and 'lower the target (bank fix)' prescriptions (cathedral_smog -3 dB, cold_slide -3 dB, omen_bells -2 dB, rooftop_rain_elegy -3 dB, sugar_glass -3 dB, clav/pluck targets). Verified in render_loop (build_loop_lines.py:192-197). Every take is normalised to 0.22 RMS with up to +6 dB make-up, and nothing reads a trim (v.trim is unused). Any gp or v trim is erased, so these cannot work as written. Bright lanes read 2-3 LUFS hotter because K-weighting lifts HF at equal RMS. The soft_knee docstring says the knee exists because a lower ceiling left peaky lanes 7-10 dB under sustained ones, so a blanket lower target brings that back. Correction: This is a loop-builder change, not a bank fix: a per-lane level offset or LUFS-based normalisation in render_loop. For knee pressure, cap make-up (for example base x 1.4 instead of x 2.0) on high-crest lanes rather than lowering every target.
- **REFUTED**: Per-kit preset edits 'in this kit' (cold_slide crystal_fm_bell index/lp, cathedral_smog cloud_fm_bell lp 6 kHz, fern_shrine lg_steel_lotus/dusty_kalimba lp, cathedral reverse take, eight_second_vespers doubled wow_c). Verified. The kit JSON has no per-lane gp override: load_kit_packs asserts that no unknown fields exist (KIT_KEYS), and render_loop always uses the bank preset's gp. Editing the bank gp changes the INSTRUMENT preset, its SIG_ renders, and every kit sharing that preset (crystal_fm_bell, cloud_fm_bell, etc.). Correction: Either add a validated per-lane 'gp' override field to KIT_KEYS and render_loop, or create new loop-only presets (for example crystal_fm_bell_soft) in the bank.
- **REFUTED**: feitico_concreto: 'open the tinnitus_siren lp'. Verified. tinnitus_siren is gen glass_pad with partials [[1,1.0],[1.004052,0.6]], air 0.0 and no lp. glass_pad has no lp stage, so its lack of HF comes from the recipe (two sine partials). Correction: To make it cut, add upper partials and/or air noise (or post_shelf) in a loop-specific siren preset; a pitch LFO would need generator support.
- **UNCERTAIN**: Pre-roll prescriptions: 'pre-roll the KEYS 0.7 s; the start wraps, and the fold handles it' and halation_wall 'one 8-beat note per take, pre-rolled 1.5 s'. Verified from code: a negative start fails the '0 <= ev[0]' assert. If it got past that, render_loop's buf[s0:e0] with negative s0 would mis-slice or raise. Pre-roll works only if written as a wrapped start (loop length minus pre-roll), which the fold then handles. halation_wall at 78 BPM: 8 beats + 1.5 s = 7.65 s, over the 6.0 s voice cap (build_loop_lines.py:170). With hold >= n the envelope never releases, so the note stops dead at 6 s. Correction: State that starts must be wrapped positive beats. For halation_wall, raise the 6 s cap (and the buffer) or split the drone into two overlapping notes.
- **UNCERTAIN**: Glide prescriptions (cold_slide, lowrider_sunset whistle, ribbon_ghost, night_drive 808): 'add a 5th event element glide_to_semitones ... (bank fix)'. Diagnosis verified: grit_808 has drop_st 12, gen_808 re-drops from +drop_st on every note, and each event is a separate render_voice call. long_glide_808 (drop_st 7) does not glide either. But a 5-element event fails 'assert len(ev) == 4' in load_kit_packs, and true legato needs phrase-level rendering (one continuous voice with a pitch curve), not per-event voices. Correction: Mark it as a build_loop_lines change (validator plus a legato phrase renderer) together with a gen_808/gen_sub pitch-curve input, not a bank-only fix.
- **UNCERTAIN**: bronze_monsoon_cycle: add a per-kit tuning field; render detuned pairs 'a few cents up' with a beat rate kept consistent across registers. Wikipedia verified (fetched): instruments are 'built in pairs that are tuned slightly apart'; this is called ombak, with 'inhale/exhale' tunings and beats ideally at 'a consistent speed for all pairs of notes in all registers'; no rate is given. From code: fractional semitones already pass the validator and work through mtof and seed_for (both float-safe), so microtuning is possible in the current format without a new field. A fixed-cents detune contradicts a consistent beat rate, because beat Hz scales with pitch. Correction: Detune each pair by a fixed Hz offset per note (semitone offset = 12*log2(1 + dHz/f)), written as fractional semitones or via an optional tuning table.
- **REFUTED**: palma_sombra: 'the accents are not flamenco'; rewrite PLUCK on pulses 3,6,8,10,12 of 'the 12-pulse cycle (2 bars of 12/8)'; A3/Bb3 rub. Verified. KEYS take 1 and PLUCK take 1 hit at beats 0, 1, 2, 2.67, 3.33 per bar. On this triplet grid those are pulses 0, 3, 6, 8, 10 of 12, which is counts 12, 3, 6, 8, 10: the bulería accents the Compás article lists starting at [12] (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Compás, fetched). One bar here is 12 pulses, not two, and the prescription only rotates the cycle. The A/Bb cluster is inside both the KEYS and PLUCK voicings (prog bar 2 has -12 and -11 adjacent). That makes it a b2 colour of the Phrygian-dominant key (inferred), so it is as intentional as seance_tape's rub. Correction: Keep the compás. If anything, vary the accents (the article's second bulería pattern accents 12, 3, 7, 8, 10) and separate the KEYS and PLUCK registers rather than moving the Bb.
- **REFUTED**: montuno_madrugada: bass 'barely anticipates'; write tumbao takes on the 'and' of 2 and on 4; voice the AIR Eb above the KEYS 9th. Verified. BASS take 1 is [1.5 +1.4] and [3 +2.4] per bar: the two tresillo offbeats, with the last note held over the barline. That matches the tumbao Wikipedia describes ('only the two offbeats of tresillo are sounded', last note 'held over the downbeat', https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tumbao, fetched). The synco metric misses it because the note on 4 is an on-beat onset. The D/Eb rub is also inside the AIR lane itself: takes 1-2 voice D4+D#4 (Live names), and prog bar 1 has 12, 14 and 15 adjacent. Correction: Keep BASS take 1. To reduce beating, re-voice the prog (9th a 7th above the b3) in both lanes; moving only the AIR does not remove it.
- **REFUTED**: organillo_roto: 'highest rub', B natural should be only a passing tone and the AIR should hold chord tones; give optical_disc_organ wow and crackle. Verified. In bar 2 (iv = Ab minor), B natural is Cb, the minor third, so it is a chord tone. The clash with Bb (the 9th) sits inside the KEYS lane itself: take 1 voices A#2+B2 in bar 2 and F3+F#3 in bar 1. The prog encodes these clusters (14 and 15 adjacent). optical_disc_organ gp already has wow 6.0, tape wow_c 6, crackle 3.0, hiss 0.01 and decim 11025. Correction: If the cluster is unwanted, re-voice the prog's 9ths above the b3s across lanes. The only new organ work is a continuous crackle bed that survives the note gating.
- **CONFIRMED**: Note names in the rub findings (G#4/A4, Bb3/B3, D5/Eb5, F4/Gb4, A3/Bb3). Verified. The pitches match the events using scientific naming (MIDI 60 = C4). Live and the plugin (qwertyRange 'C3-E4' for 60-76) use MIDI 60 = C3.
- **CONFIRMED**: Modal lanes negative L/R correlation (feitico_concreto, bronze AIR, heaven_static, omen_bells, carousel 'slightly negative -0.10'). Verified mechanism: gen_modal draws an independent rng.uniform phase per side per mode (sigbank_dsp.py:517), and glass_pad does the same at line 723. Measured: feitico pluck1 corr -0.18, mono sum -3.9 dB; carousel_after_hours pluck1 corr -0.27, mono sum -4.4 dB.
- **CONFIRMED**: halo_mosh seven_sun_saw renders bit-identical L=R. Verified: gp spread 0.0 in a 7-voice supersaw, and LL_halo_mosh_keys1 L==R exactly.
- **CONFIRMED**: Lanes 'drop to digital silence between hits' (floor -96/-97 dBFS); prescription 'add room tails'. Verified: -96/-97 dBFS is the 16-bit TPDF dither floor. Root cause in render_loop (line 179): each note is gated to hold + at most 250 ms, ignoring the preset's v.rel (eight_hertz 1500 ms, first_light_bellpad 2000 ms, crystal_fm_bell 1200 ms). The loops are drier than the same presets in INSTRUMENT mode.
- **REFUTED**: glasshouse_dawn: wet_rim_seraph 'with a 180 ms attack'. Verified: 180 ms is v.atk (plugin AHDSR), which render_loop never applies. gen_sub with no t60 uses env_adsr a=0.004, so the loop render has a 4 ms attack. The rest of the finding (mono sine, no HF, about 0.59 onsets/beat) stands. Correction: Remove the 180 ms attack claim for the loop render.
- **REFUTED**: Swap target 'cold_glass (glass_pad, attack about 0.3 s)'. Verified: cold_glass gp attack is 0.5 s. Correction: Use 0.5 s, and note it will also swell late on short notes unless pre-rolled.
- **CONFIRMED**: External sources: 40 Lo-Fi 4400 Hz on the 'Headlines' bass; Frank Dukes keeps analog baseline noise. Fetched both. SOS quotes 40 using Lo-Fi on an Xpand! bass, 'I pulled the sample rate down to 4400Hz'. The SOS Frank Dukes p2 passage is about capturing analogue synths' baseline noise in their sampled instrument (The Prince VST), not vinyl or loop beds.

## 6. Code audit lane (raw findings)

I audited the work from 2a33674..e94ae83 (SAMPLES Stage 1, the LOOPS latch, loop FX, archive trims, the CI gates, the stretch patch) and the private AppData indexer. I checked HISE behaviour against the 4.1.0 tag with gh api raw reads, saved to /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-noxvitae/0e6ab598-c428-4549-a613-b529408ad567/scratchpad/wf_mystic/hise410/. Rendered audio and index.json were analysed with scripts in /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-noxvitae/0e6ab598-c428-4549-a613-b529408ad567/scratchpad/wf_mystic/.

These parts look correct at 4.1.0:
- SampleRouter grid math: Sixteenth=11, grid index counted from ppq 0, transport callbacks run before render.
- The fix that cancels a delayed note-on when its note-off comes first.
- Key match through the stretcher: pitch comes from uptimeDelta.
- The NumQuarters int-cast workaround and the device-rate correction.
- RepeatMode 3 = KillSecondOldestNote.
- The name-collision gate: it finds no parameter or loop-variable collisions either.

The 15 findings below are real defects in the new code, or in the indexer and render output, plus one CI-gate idea. Each finding says whether it is verified by source, verified by measurement, or inferred. None was run in Live.

Top issues:
1. The SAMPLES FORM filter (LOOP / ONE-SHOT / ALL) is dead. Math.floor returns a double, so the hit id becomes "f1.0", which never matches the handler.
2. LOOPS latch goes stale after a kit change or feel flip. All voices are killed, but the latch state is not reset.
3. RESCAN keeps array positions while the item list changes, so PLAY KEYS, SIMILAR and the selection silently swap to other sounds.
4. One-shot auditions pitched down for key match are cut off at the file length, so 808 tails are lost.
5. Tine EP renders start every note with a DC step. 33 of 44 SIG files are affected, and in LL_neon_perreo_keys1 the loop jumps by 0.845 FS at the seam every cycle.

- **[major/high] SAMPLES FORM pills (LOOP / ONE-SHOT / ALL) never change the filter** (SAMPLES UI: hise_project/ResearchFacility/Scripts/SamplesPage.js:884, :1057). Evidence: Verified by source. SamplesPage.js:884 returns "f" + Math.floor((x - 12) / 77.4). At the 4.1.0 tag Math.floor is `var floor(var value) { return std::floor((double)value); }`, a double var (https://github.com/christophhart/HISE/blob/4.1.0/hi_scripting/scripting/engine/JavascriptEngineMathObject.cpp). A string plus a double goes through JUCE serialiseDouble, which writes integral doubles as "1.0". The project already hit this in f41a786, where the kit pager read "1.0-7.0/64". So the id is "f0.0", "f1.0" or "f2.0" (length 4), and the handler at :1057 requires h.length == 2. No other branch matches, so the click does nothing. The same root cause makes the hover ids "g5.0", "ge4.0" and "sp1.0" (:872, :893, :1271) never equal the paint-side "g"+k, "ge"+k and "sp"+sp (:962, :997, :1338), so those pills never highlight. Their clicks still work because those handlers use parseInt/substring. Scenario: Open SAMPLES and click ONE-SHOT. SM.form stays 2 (ALL) and the list does not change. The user can never split loops from one-shots: SM.form only changes when user.json view data is restored. Fix: Build the ids from integers, for example "f" + Math.round(Math.floor(...)) (Math.round returns an int at 4.1.0). Do the same for g, ge and sp, or match with h.indexOf("f") == 0 && h.length >= 2 && parseInt(h.substring(1, 2)). Add a selfTest check: smpRailHit(50, 160) == "f0".
- **[major/high] LOOPS latch: after a kit change or a feel flip, latched keys stay lit and the next press is swallowed** (LOOPS latch: Interface.js:3768-3781 (loadKit), :605-630 (applyFeel), :3905, :4017; KeyShift.js:72-81). Evidence: Verified by code path, not run in Live. loadKit calls LoopSampler.loadSampleMap and then, with S.loopQ = -1, applyFeel, which calls LoopSampler.setTimestretchOptions (:627). At 4.1.0 both go through killAllVoicesAndCall: ScriptingApi.cpp Sampler::loadSampleMap, and ModulatorSampler::setTimestretchOptions (https://github.com/christophhart/HISE/blob/4.1.0/hi_core/hi_sampler/sampler/ModulatorSampler.cpp). KillStateHandler then kills the whole main synth chain: KillStateHandler.cpp:81 and :141 call getMainSynthChain()->killAllVoices() (https://github.com/christophhart/HISE/blob/4.1.0/hi_core/hi_core/KillStateHandler.cpp). Only setMode and setLatch bump Globals.latchEpoch or clear latchMask. So KeyShift.js:74-81 still holds LATCH[n] for a voice that no longer exists, and treats the next press as the stop press: noteOffByEventId on the dead id, then ignoreEvent. syncLatchKeys keeps the tint lit from the stale mask. The same happens when the host tempo crosses the 0.7 / 1.45 feel thresholds (onTempo at :3905, and the check about once a second at :4017). Scenario: LOOPS, LATCH ON. Press A and W (K1 and K2 loop). Click another kit: the loops go silent but C3 and C#3 stay lit. Press A: nothing plays, because the press is eaten as a stop. Press A again and it starts. Every lane that was latched needs a double press after any kit change or tempo-threshold crossing. Fix: In loadKit, and in applyFeel when it actually calls setTimestretchOptions, bump Globals.latchEpoch, set Globals.latchMask = 0 and call clearLoopHeld(). For an Arcade-style workflow, a better fix: remember the latched lane notes and re-trigger them once the new kit map is in, polling isNoteNumberMapped the way SAMPLES does.
- **[major/high] RESCAN swaps the PLAY KEYS, SIMILAR and selected sound because array positions survive an index reload** (SAMPLES state: SamplesPage.js:209-281 (smpLoadIndex), :1419-1427, :1463, :705, :603-611). Evidence: Verified by source. smpLoadIndex replaces SM.items (:228) but keeps SM.playKeys, SM.like, SM.playIt, SM.want and SM.sel. These are positions in the OLD array. smpRefilter(true) (:274) then re-selects via selId, which is also an old position. The indexer sorts by source, then path (AppData tools/samples_index.py:263-273), so one new download shifts every later index. After that:
- The clock reloads PLAY KEYS from SM.items[SM.playKeys] (:1463), and smpKeysChanged does the same (:705); PlayItem then saves the wrong id (:680).
- If the index shrinks, the old position is undefined and the clock or refilter throws (base.sim on undefined, :425).
- smpLoadIndex also calls smpLoadUser (:244), which overwrites hearts and plays still waiting for the 1 s save.
- smpRefilter never clears a queued SM.want, so a refilter inside the 80 ms wait plays slot r of the old map while the UI marks a different item (:603-611). Scenario: PLAY KEYS is on an 808, either picked now or restored from the set. Download one new Splice sound that sorts before it and press RESCAN. Then change PITCH or the song key: Sample Play loads the neighbouring sound, and the Live set now saves that sound's id. Fix: Keep ids, not positions: playKeysId, likeId, the selected id and the playing id. After smpLoadIndex, map them back through SM.idx and drop any that are gone. Stop the audition and clear SM.want before replacing SM.items, and clear SM.want in smpRefilter. Save pending user data (smpSaveUser) before smpLoadUser, or merge the two.
- **[major/high] One-shot auditions pitched down for key match are cut early, losing 808 tails** (SAMPLES audition: SamplesPage.js:1443, :617, :1324, :451). Evidence: Verified by source. For hits, the clock sends the note-off at startAt + it.dur + 0.05 s (:1443), where dur is the file length (samples_index.py:351). The zone is transposed by Root = slot - shift (:451). Sample Hits has OneShot="0" and a 50 ms release (XmlPresetBackups/ResearchFacility.xml, Sample Hits Env). A shift of -s semitones therefore plays for dur x 2^(s/12) but is released at dur. IN KEY shifts range from -6 to +5 semitones, and OCT -1 adds another -12. The playhead (:1324) also ends early. Scenario: Song key C, an F 808 of 2.0 s shifted -5 st: it would ring 2.67 s but is released at 2.05 s. With OCT -1, every one-shot audition stops at half its real length. Fix: Set offAt = startAt + it.dur * Math.pow(2, -smpShift(it) / 12) + release, and use the same factor for the playhead. Alternatively set Sample Hits to OneShot and skip the note-off.
- **[major/high] Tine EP renders start every note with a DC step, and 84 of 1,024 loop takes click at the loop seam** (Render pipeline: scripts/sigbank_dsp.py:309 (gen_tine_ep, touched by ba64ded); scripts/build_loop_lines.py:188, :240). Evidence: Verified by measurement on the installed renders (scipy). gen_tine_ep subtracts mean(x[:2048]) from the whole note after the attack envelope, so sample 0 steps from 0 to -mean. The pickup x^2 term makes the mean positive, and the measured starts are indeed negative.
- SIG files: 33 of 44 tine_ep renders have |x[0]| above 5 percent of peak, worst SIG_perreo_keys_36.wav at 0.151 FS (24 percent of peak). Other generators are clean except supersaw (21 of 139) and 808 (7 of 28).
- LOOPS: the loop seam has LoopXFade="0" (:240) and no envelope. LL_neon_perreo_keys1.wav ends at 0.000 and starts at -0.845 FS; keys2 starts at -0.499, keys3 and keys4 at -0.27 and -0.28.
- 84 of 1,024 LL takes have a seam step above 0.05 FS: supersaw 19, 808 15, fm_bell 8, prepared 5, tine 4. Scenario: Neon Perreo kit with KEYS 1 latched: a loud click on beat 1 of every 2-bar cycle. Played as instruments, Perreo Keys and the other tine EPs tick on each note-on; the 1 ms attack only shortens the step, it does not remove it. Fix: Drop the constant subtraction, since the 30 Hz hp already removes DC, or multiply it by the attack envelope. In render_loop, after the fold, ramp about 2 ms at each end toward the seam value, or write a few-ms LoopXFade. Add a CI check: |x[0] - x[-1]| < 0.01 FS and |x[0]| < 0.01 FS for every LL take and SIG file.
- **[minor/high] Backspace (previous sound) works once, then skips a sound or does nothing** (SAMPLES shortcuts: SamplesPage.js:789-808, :562-571). Evidence: Verified by trace. smpBack removes the current id (:801), plays hist[i] (:802), then removes the last element again (:803) on the assumption that smpPlayRes logged it twice. But smpNoteHistory only pushes when the last id differs, and after :801 the last id IS the sound being played. So :803 deletes the current sound from the history. Scenario: Play X, then A, then B (hist [X, A, B]). Backspace plays A and leaves hist [X]. A second Backspace does nothing, because i = -1; it should play X. With [W, X, A, B], the second press jumps to W and skips X. Fix: Delete line 803, or remove the current id, call smpPlayRes, and let smpNoteHistory dedupe.
- **[minor/high] SAMPLES counters show decimals: '4.0/2400' and '13.0-24.0 of 2,400'** (SAMPLES UI: SamplesPage.js:1217-1218, :439, :559, :1128, :1246; Interface.js:959). Evidence: Verified by source. A Results click computes the row with Math.floor (:1217), so r, SM.sel and SM.want become doubles. smpEnsureVisible and smpRefilter set SM.top = Math.floor(...) * 12 (:439, :559). The header prints (SM.sel + 1) + "/" (Interface.js:959), and Results prints (SM.top + 1) + "-" + Math.min(...) (:1128). At 4.1.0, Math.range, Math.min and Math.max return an int only when every argument is an int, so the double carries through Up/Down stepping. This is the same class as the kit pager fixed in f41a786. Scenario: Click the 4th row: the header LCD reads "4.0/2400". Page down: the tape reads "13.0-24.0 of 2,400". Fix: Wrap derived row indices in Math.round (:1217, :439, :559, :1246), or format them with Engine.doubleToString(v, 0).
- **[minor/high] PLAY KEYS puts 808 and bass one-shots below the Mac keys: silent on A..; and only above root on the visible piano** (SAMPLES PLAY KEYS: SamplesPage.js:668-675; Interface.js:2737 (Keys LowKey 36)). Evidence: Verified on index.json. For hits, smpSetPlayKeys sets Root rt, LoKey rt-24 and HiKey rt+24. The indexer gives 808 and bass roots of pc+24 to pc+36 (samples_index.py:129-160). In the current index, 93 of 94 keyed 808/BASS one-shots have HiKey below 60. The default QWERTY range 60-76 therefore plays nothing, and the on-screen piano (36-84) only reaches them 7 to 24 semitones above root, never at their own pitch. Scenario: Pick an F 808 (root 29), turn on PLAY KEYS and press A..K: silence. On the piano the lowest key, C2, already plays it +7 st. Doc 21b acceptance test 3 (tuner on C, F, G) needs an external controller or 3x '['. Fix: Fold hit zones by octaves so they play inside 48-72, as the loop branch does with C3 = song key, with Root offset by the same number of octaves so the pitch stays right. Or show the playable range and offer a one-click octave shift.
- **[minor/high] Loops that need more than 128 quarters play at the wrong tempo (NumQuarters clamp)** (SAMPLES engine: SamplesPage.js:475, :46, :112-117, :1322). Evidence: Counts verified on index.json; tempo error worked out from the code, not heard in Live. smpLoopNq = min(128, nq x feel x deviceRate / fileRate). Auto feel guards the doubling step (:116), but the rate correction alone can push past 128, and forced speeds (SMP_SPEED_M) have no guard. Of 2,007 indexed loops: on a 48 kHz device, 3 exceed 128 always and 35 at 1/2x; on a 96 kHz device, 35 and 481. With the clamp, the stretcher assumes too few quarters, so the loop runs fast. The playhead (:1322) uses the unclamped length. Scenario: A 32-bar, 44.1 kHz loop (nq 128) in Live at 48 kHz: 139.3 is clamped to 128, so the loop runs about 8 percent fast, ending about 10 quarters early each cycle, and drifts off Live's bar grid. Fix: When the corrected value exceeds 128, halve the feel (double time) or mark the row and skip it in IN TEMPO. Apply the same guard to forced speeds, or cap these loops at 16 bars in the indexer.
- **[minor/high] Results break once the index passes 8,192 sounds, and the indexer's similarity step uses O(n^2) memory** (SAMPLES sort: SamplesPage.js:40, :432-435; AppData tools/samples_index.py:358-381). Evidence: Verified by source. smpRefilter packs each key as sortKey x 8192 + item index and unpacks it with % 8192, with no guard on SM.items.length. The index holds 2,400 sounds today (1,376 Splice plus 1,024 LL). The indexer builds several dense n x n float arrays (T @ T.T, jac, d, kcomp, r, bclose, score); at 20,000 items each float64 matrix is 3.2 GB. Scenario: A library of 9,000 downloads: item 8,200 is listed, named and played as item 8. A large RESCAN runs out of memory and exits non-zero. Fix: Sort [key, index] pairs, or sort an index array with a comparator. Compute similarity in row blocks with a running top-12 per row.
- **[minor/high] Shift+Backspace hides a sound forever: there is no unhide and the help text does not mention it** (SAMPLES shortcuts: SamplesPage.js:778-787, :1481, :1493, :1015, :1076-1089). Evidence: Verified by grep. smpHide sets SM.hidden[id] = 1 and saves it to user.json (:165). smpPass drops hidden items (:351). CLEAR and every other path leave SM.hidden alone, and the rail help (:1015) lists only plain Backspace. Scenario: A slip on Shift while pressing Backspace (previous sound) removes the current sound from every future search. The only way back is editing AppData/SampleBrowser/user.json by hand. Fix: Add a HIDDEN chip or an UNHIDE ALL entry in the rail, keep the last hidden id for an undo, and add the shortcut to the help text.
- **[minor/high] FIX KEY stores the song's MAJ/MIN as the sound's mode, which can make the key match worse** (SAMPLES FIX KEY: SamplesPage.js:1041, :758-768, :101-102, :866-875). Evidence: Verified by source. The grid calls smpFixKey(smpSelItem(), gk, SM.songMode). The grid has no mode cells, and clicking MAJ/MIN while it is open just closes it. smpShift then chooses the relative major/minor target from the stored mode. Scenario: Song in C major. An A minor loop mis-tagged as C is fixed to A: it is stored as A major, so the shift becomes +3 and the loop plays in C minor against the C major song. The correct result is A minor with a shift of 0. Fix: Add MAJ / MIN / ? cells to the fix grid, defaulting to the item's current mode.
- **[minor/high] RAW audition still colours every sample: the Air EQ shelves stay on** (SAMPLES FX: SamplesPage.js:1505-1513; XmlPresetBackups/ResearchFacility.xml:66). Evidence: Verified by source. smpRawFx bypasses Master Filter, Drive, Chorus, Space Delay and Master Reverb, but not "Air EQ". Air EQ band 0 is +2.5 dB LowShelf at 120 Hz and band 1 is +2.0 dB HighShelf at 9 kHz. The band order (Gain, Freq, Q, Enabled, Type) and the type values LowShelf=2, HighShelf=3 come from CurveEq.h at 4.1.0 (https://github.com/christophhart/HISE/blob/4.1.0/hi_core/hi_modules/effects/fx/CurveEq.h). The archive-trim commit bfbaef1 also refers to this 2.5 dB shelf. Scenario: Auditioning 808s and basses: +2.5 dB below about 120 Hz versus the dragged original, so samples sound different in the plug-in than in Live, and loudness matching is off. Fix: In smpRawFx, also call Synth.getEffect("Air EQ").setBypassed(raw).
- **[minor/medium] Indexer reads Splice's WAL-mode database with immutable=1, so the newest downloads lose key, BPM and form** (AppData indexer (private, personal-only): tools/adapters/splice.py:20). Evidence: Mechanism verified, impact inferred. splice.py opens "file:...?mode=ro&immutable=1". sounds.db is in WAL mode: sounds.db-wal (8,272 bytes) and sounds.db-shm exist (file names and sizes listed only, contents not read). Test in scratch with python 3.12 sqlite3: 5 rows committed only to the WAL are visible with mode=ro and invisible with immutable=1 (0 rows). The SQLite docs say immutable skips all locking and change detection (https://www.sqlite.org/uri.html). Files with no row become orphans: no key, BPM from the filename only, form guessed. Loops can then index as one-shots and skip key matching. The current report shows 7 orphans; whether they are WAL rows is not known. Scenario: Download sounds in Splice and press RESCAN right away: the new sounds show NO KEY and no BPM, or as HIT, until Splice checkpoints its WAL. Fix: Open with mode=ro only (works with the existing -shm), or copy db, -wal and -shm to a temp dir and open the copy. Report the orphan count per run.
- **[idea/medium] Licence CI gate knows one vendor only, is case-sensitive and skips docs/** (CI: scripts/ci/check_licence_boundary.py:15-16, :22). Evidence: Verified by source. PATTERNS holds only "/Splice/" and "sounds.db", matched with git grep -F (case-sensitive), and the scopes leave out docs/ and design/ even though docs/21b is tracked. It passes today. Personal-only libraries the user might sample later (Keyscape, Omnisphere: personal-only, never in the public repo) are not covered, and an absolute /Users/ path in a SampleMaps XML would pass. Scenario: A future map or doc that names ~/splice/... in lower case, or a Spectrasonics library path, gets committed and CI stays green. Fix: Use a data-driven deny list (vendor path fragments, library names, SampleBrowser), git grep -i, and add docs/ and design/ to the scopes. Fail on any FileName="/Users/ in SampleMaps.

### Fact-check (16 items checked by an adversarial verifier)

- **CONFIRMED**: 1. SAMPLES FORM pills (LOOP / ONE-SHOT / ALL) never change the filter (SamplesPage.js:884, :1057). Verified by source. :884 returns "f" + Math.floor((x - 12) / 77.4). At 4.1.0 Math.floor is `return std::floor((double)value);`, a double var (saved JavascriptEngineMathObject.cpp:276). Integral doubles stringify as "1.0". The project's own Live test recorded this in f41a786 ("Kit pager read 1.0-7.0/64"). The click branch at :1057 requires h.length == 2, and no other branch matches "f1.0". Same root cause for the hover ids: "g" + (row * 4 + col) at :872, "ge" + gi at :893 and "sp" + Math.floor(...) at :1271. Paint compares them with int loop variables ("g" + k at :962, "f" + k at :989, "ge" + k at :997, "sp" + sp at :1338), so those never highlight. The g, ge and sp clicks still work because they use parseInt.
- **CONFIRMED**: 2. LOOPS latch goes stale after a kit change or a feel flip (Interface.js loadKit :3768-3781, applyFeel :605-630; KeyShift.js:72-81). Verified by code path, not in Live. loadKit sets S.loopQ = -1 and calls applyFeel, so setTimestretchOptions runs on every kit change, plus loadSampleMap when the map differs. ModulatorSampler::setTimestretchOptions calls killAllVoicesAndCall(f, true) (saved ModulatorSampler.cpp:1035-1063). From the scripting thread this goes through KillStateHandler, which calls getMainSynthChain()->killAllVoices() for an instrument build (KillStateHandler.cpp:81). Only setLatch (:3847-3855) and setMode (:3882-3884) bump Globals.latchEpoch. I also checked that the next press really is swallowed: noteOffDelayedByEventId does not throw on a missing or dead id (ScriptingApi.cpp:5352-5397, else branch calls setArtificialTimestamp), so KeyShift reaches ignoreEvent(true). syncLatchKeys (:4018) keeps the stale mask lit. The tempo path is right too: applyFeel runs every 30 ticks of the 33 ms Visualiser timer (about 1 s) and from onTempo.
- **CONFIRMED**: 3. RESCAN keeps array positions across an index reload (SamplesPage.js:209-281, :1463, :705, :603-611). Verified by source. smpLoadIndex replaces SM.items (:228) and SM.idx, but leaves SM.playKeys, SM.like, SM.playIt, SM.want and SM.sel alone. smpRefilter(true) computes selId from the OLD SM.res, then indexes into the new res. smpKeysChanged (:705), the clock check at :1463 and smpEnter all call smpSetPlayKeys(SM.playKeys) against the new array, which writes the new item's id to PlayItem. The indexer sorts paths within each source (samples_index.py:263-273), so one insertion shifts every later position. If the index shrinks, SM.items[pos] is undefined and smpLoopNq / base.sim throw. smpRefilter sets SM.page = -1 but leaves SM.want and the old SM.mk marker, so smpMapReady stays true and smpStartWanted plays slot r of the old map.
- **CONFIRMED**: 4. One-shot auditions pitched down for key match are released at the file length (SamplesPage.js:1443). Verified by source and XML. In the clock, offAt = startAt + SM.items[SM.playIt].dur + 0.05 (:1443). The zone Root = slot - smpShift(it) (:451), and Sample Hits has PitchTracking="1", OneShot="0" and Sample Hits Env Release="50" (XmlPresetBackups/ResearchFacility.xml:456 on). smpShift returns d in -6..+5, plus SM.semi (-6..+6) and SM.oct * 12 (:96-106). The arithmetic holds: -5 st gives 2.0 x 2^(5/12) = 2.67 s, and the release starts at 2.05 s. The hit playhead uses it.dur (:1324).
- **CONFIRMED**: 5. Tine EP DC step at note start, and loop-seam clicks in LL takes (sigbank_dsp.py:309; build_loop_lines.py:188, :240). Mechanism verified by source and measurement. sigbank_dsp.py:309 subtracts mean(x[:2048]) after the 1.5 ms amp envelope, so sample 0 steps to -mean. The 30 Hz hp at :310 passes the step and lets it decay over about 5 ms. My recount over all 2,752 SIG files (verify_code/v_dc.py): every Perreo Keys file starts near -0.15 FS (SIG_perreo_keys_36: -0.151 FS, 23.7 percent of peak). LL seams (v_seam.py): neon_perreo_keys1 x0 = -0.845 to end 0.000; keys2 x0 = -0.498; keys3 x0 = -0.269. 84 of 1,024 takes have a raw seam step above 0.05 FS. The step is not only at the seam: inside LL_neon_perreo_keys1 the chord onset at beat 4 has the same 0.847 step (v_tine_ll.py), because render_loop applies no attack ramp to each note.
- **CONFIRMED**: 6. Backspace (previous sound) works once, then skips (SamplesPage.js:789-808). Verified by trace. With hist [X, A, B]: :801 removes B, smpPlayRes plays A, and smpNoteHistory does not push because the last entry is already A. Then :803 removes A, leaving [X]. The next press has i = -1 and does nothing. With [W, X, A, B] the second press plays W. HISE Array.removeElement takes an index, as used here.
- **CONFIRMED**: 7. SAMPLES counters show decimals ('4.0/2400', '13.0-24.0 of 2,400'). Verified by source. The Results click sets r = SM.top + Math.floor(...) (:1217-1218), a double, and smpPlayRes stores it in SM.sel. Interface.js:959 prints (SM.sel + 1) + "/". smpEnsureVisible (:567) and smpRefilter (:439) set SM.top = Math.floor(...) * SMP_ROWS, a double, and :1128 prints (SM.top + 1) and Math.min(int, double), which returns a double. The f41a786 Live test shows the stringification.
- **CONFIRMED**: 8. PLAY KEYS puts 808 and bass one-shots below the Mac keys (SamplesPage.js:668-675). Verified on index.json (verify_code/v_idx.py, v_idx2.py). Hits get LoKey rt-24, HiKey rt+24, Root rt. The KeyShift QWERTY zone is 60-76, octave shift is limited to -3..+3 (Interface.js:2780-2790), and the piano LowKey is 36 (Interface.js:2742). 93 keyed 808/BASS one-shots have root 24-34, so HiKey is 48-58, below 60, and they are silent on the unshifted Mac keys.
- **CONFIRMED**: 9. NumQuarters clamp at 128 makes long loops run fast (SamplesPage.js:475, :112-117). Counts re-run and identical: at 48 kHz, 3 loops exceed 128 at 1x and 35 at 2x feel; at 96 kHz, 35 and 481. The auto-feel guard at :116 includes the rate factor, but forced SMP_SPEED_M does not. I re-derived the error from the project's own L3 premise (file consumed at the device rate, syncer sourced from the file rate at ModulatorSampler.cpp:1226-1228): actual tempo = host x (nq / nqOpt) x (device / file). A 128-quarter 44.1 kHz loop at 48 kHz clamped to 128 runs 8.8 percent fast and ends about 10.4 quarters early. Not heard in Live.
- **CONFIRMED**: 10. Results break past 8,192 sounds; indexer similarity uses O(n^2) memory. Verified by source. SMP_PACK = 8192 (:40); keys are sortKey * 8192 + i and unpacked with % 8192, with no length guard (:430-435). The index holds 2,400 items today. samples_index.py:358-381 builds n x n matrices: inter, jac, kcomp, r and bclose as float32; d as int64; score as float64; and argsort as int64. At 20,000 items each 8-byte matrix is 3.2 GB.
- **CONFIRMED**: 11. Shift+Backspace hides a sound forever, with no unhide. Verified by grep. SM.hidden is only set at :781, saved at :165, restored at :179 and checked at :351. Nothing clears it, CLEAR (:1076-1089) leaves it alone, and the help text at :1015 lists only Backspace.
- **CONFIRMED**: 12. FIX KEY stores the song's MAJ/MIN as the sound's mode. Verified by source. The grid click calls smpFixKey(smpSelItem(), gk, SM.songMode). While SM.grid is set, smpRailHit returns only g* or gclose (:866-875), so the MAJ/MIN pill just closes the grid. The trace holds: in a C major song, an item fixed to A is stored as major, so smpShift gives d = 3 and the A minor loop plays as C minor. Fixed as minor, it would get tg = 9 and d = 0.
- **CONFIRMED**: 13. RAW audition leaves the Air EQ shelves on (smpRawFx, SamplesPage.js:1505-1513). Verified by source. XML :66 has CurveEq "Air EQ" Bypassed=0: Band0 Gain 2.5, Band1 Freq 120, Band3 Enabled 1, Band4 Type 2 = LowShelf; Band5 Gain 2.0, Band6 9000, Band9 Type 3 = HighShelf. The band order is Gain, Freq, Q, Enabled, Type, and the type enum is LowPass 0, HighPass 1, LowShelf 2, HighShelf 3 (saved CurveEq.h:68-86). No script references "Air EQ".
- **CONFIRMED**: 14. Splice adapter opens a WAL database with immutable=1 (AppData tools/adapters/splice.py:20). Mechanism verified. The URI is "?mode=ro&immutable=1", and sounds.db-wal (8,272 bytes) and sounds.db-shm exist (names and sizes only). Re-run in scratch (verify_code/v_wal.py): 5 rows committed only to the WAL are seen by mode=ro (5) and not by immutable=1 (0). index_report.txt shows orphans 7. Impact on real rows is inferred.
- **CONFIRMED**: 15. Licence CI gate: one vendor, case-sensitive, skips docs/ (scripts/ci/check_licence_boundary.py). Verified by source. PATTERNS is ["/Splice/", "sounds.db"] with git grep -F (case-sensitive), and SCOPES has no docs/ or design/. docs/21b_samples_stage1.md is tracked. git grep -i over docs and design for the path finds nothing today, so this is a gap, not a current leak.
- **REFUTED**: Summary top-5 item 5 ('33 of 44 SIG files are affected'). A full recount over all 2,752 SIG files (verify_code/v_dc.py) gives 83 of 302 tine_ep files with |x0| > 5 percent of peak. The loop half of the line (keys1 seam 0.845 FS) is correct. Correction: Replace with: '83 of 302 Tine EP renders start with a DC offset (Perreo Keys at about -0.15 FS). Each tine chord onset inside a loop steps by up to 0.85 FS: the seam and beat 4 of LL_neon_perreo_keys1.' Scripts: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-noxvitae/0e6ab598-c428-4549-a613-b529408ad567/scratchpad/wf_mystic/verify_code/v_dc.py, v_seam.py, v_seam3.py, v_tine.py, v_tine_ll.py, v_idx.py, v_idx2.py, v_wal.py, v_x0.py.

## 7. UX and gaps lane (raw findings)

Lane: what is missing or not working for the user. I read Interface.js all the way through, plus SamplesPage.js, KeyShift.js, SampleRouter.js, the XML module tree, FactoryBank.js, LoopBank.js, the loop-kit packs and the sample maps. I compared all of that with docs 13 to 22. I changed nothing in the repo. My scratch script is /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-noxvitae/0e6ab598-c428-4549-a613-b529408ad567/scratchpad/wf_mystic/maps_audit.py.

Bottom line: the plug-in does three things today. It plays one sampled layer with a sine sub, it has a working (but unused) mod matrix, and it runs 64 tempo-synced loop kits plus a SAMPLES browser.

Most of what the user asked for is still design-only:
- Key and BPM scan (doc 20): nothing in the instrument.
- Vocal hook (doc 22): zero code. Its disk-space blocker is gone: 100 GiB free today.
- Mono, legato, glide and arp (doc 16 stages 4 and 5): not built.
- Loop snap, song key and shapers (doc 15): not built.
- Porcelain Lab layout and FX page (doc 17): not built.
- Stage visual (doc 18b): stage 2 and 3 not built.
- Texture chain (doc 13: Submerge, tape, convolution, round robins): not built.

Biggest problems the user will actually hit:
- Hand-played loops never snap to the bar or key.
- In LOOPS you cannot play a melody over the loops.
- No factory preset uses the mod matrix, so sounds feel static.
- There is no way to save your own sound, and loading a preset wipes your edits.
- In LOOPS the macro and ATTACK/RELEASE knobs show values that are not what you hear.
- The SAMPLES "RAW" audition still runs through a hidden EQ boost.

Also, installed_build.txt suggests the copy in Live is 60875b6, four commits behind HEAD.

Verified means read in code, XML or files, or measured. Inferred is marked as such.

- **[major/high] Loop lanes start unquantized: no SNAP, so hand-played or latched loops drift off the bar and off each other** (LOOPS (doc 15)). Evidence: Verified. SampleRouter.js L50-66 delays note-ons to the next bar or beat, but only on MIDI channel 15, which carries SAMPLES auditions. KeyShift.js has no delayEvent: its latch code (L69-90) passes loop-zone notes straight through. Interface.js has no SNAP control anywhere; LOOPS UI is lines 1079-1140. Doc 15 'Staying in time' (delayEvent quantize, Snap LOOP/BAR/BEAT/OFF) and its LoopRouter.js were never built. The ScriptProcessors folder has KeyShift, SampleRouter and three Smp*In scripts, but no LoopRouter. Scenario: Live at 98 BPM, transport running, LATCH on. The user taps K1 on beat 1, then B2 about 200 ms late. The bass loop stays 200 ms behind the keys loop for as long as it runs. Neither loop lines up with Live's bar, because the sampler has no phase lock (doc 15 Part 2a, verified). Fix: Reuse the SampleRouter grid code for loop-zone notes in LOOPS mode, as doc 15 designed. Notes 60-75 get Message.delayEvent up to the next multiple of kit.quarters (Snap LOOP), with BAR, BEAT and OFF as options. Add a SNAP stepper to the LOOPS card. While a lane waits for its start, draw a T.warn 'queued' LED on the key, the same way SamplesPage shows smpDelay.
- **[major/high] LOOPS has no song key: 64 kits are fixed in 22 different keys and cannot follow the song or each other** (LOOPS (doc 15 Key sync) and doc 20). Evidence: Verified. No script anywhere in Scripts/ calls setCoarseDetune, addPitchFade or setTransposeAmount; grep finds only SampleRouter's delayEvent. Kit keys in LoopBank.js, counted: A minor 6, F# minor 5, Db major 5, E/F/B/D/C minor 4 each, plus a dozen more. SAMPLES already has a SONG KEY label (SamplesPage.js L695-729) and the relative major/minor shift rule (smpShift, L95-105), but LOOPS never reads either. The header in LOOPS only prints the kit's own static BPM and key (Interface.js L955). Scenario: The song is in F minor and the user loads Neon Perreo (F# minor). Every lane is a semitone off. Layering Night Drive (A minor) with Cold Slide (F minor) is impossible. Fix: Make SongKey one global value shared by SAMPLES, LOOPS, the keyboard tint and the preview. In a non-deferred router, apply shift = wrap(songRoot - kitRoot) to the range -6..+5 with Message.setCoarseDetune on new lane notes, and addPitchFade on running ones (doc 15). Reuse the smpShift relative-key logic. Show a ROOT LCD. Doc 15 left one thing unverified: whether the event pitch factor reaches the stretcher. Check it by ear first.
- **[major/high] In LOOPS you cannot play a melody over the loops, and the lane instruments are never named** (LOOPS, melody workflow). Evidence: Verified. setMode bypasses Voice A and Sub whenever the mode is not 0 (Interface.js L3870-3871). The Loop Lines map only has zones on notes 60-75, so every other key is silent in LOOPS. Each kit has a 'lanes' list of real factory preset ids (LoopBank.js), and all 124 of those ids exist in FactoryBank. The UI only draws the generic names KEYS/BASS/PLUCK/AIR (Interface.js L1126-1136) and the lower-third text 'KEYS C3 / BASS E3 / PLUCK G#3 / AIR C4' (L2110). Scenario: The user latches the Seance Tape lanes and wants to play its toy-box piano melody on C5. Nothing sounds. They need a second plug-in instance, and they have to guess that the PLUCK lane is 'toy_box_piano'. Fix: Split the keyboard in LOOPS. The loop zone stays at 60-75, and notes 36-59 and 76-96 play Voice A (keep it unbypassed and route by note in the router). Print each lane's preset name under its lane label. Add a 'PLAY THIS LANE' chip that runs loadPreset(findPreset(kit.lanes[i])) without leaving LOOPS. That gives instruments ready to play melodies in the kit's key.
- **[major/high] Key and BPM scan (doc 20) is not in the instrument: no tempo or key LCD, no scale lock, previews and strum hard-wired to C minor** (Key and tempo (doc 20), melody helpers (doc 14 ranks 2 and 10)). Evidence: Verified.
- In PLAY, SHAPE and MOD the header draws no host BPM and no key (Interface.js L892-1006). Doc 20 Stage 1 asked for an LCD at [622,8,104,36]. LOOPS shows only the kit's static values (L955).
- There is no Performer.js (no key or scale lock, no white-key map).
- FactoryBank entries have no 'key' field. Doc 20 Stage 0 required build_signature_bank.py to write one.
- PREVIEW plays fixed minor-7 templates at fixed roots, falling back to 92 BPM (L719-760).
- Click-to-strum uses STRUM_SCALE = C minor pentatonic (L1735, L2147-2160).
- Only SAMPLES has a host-BPM LCD and a manual key picker (SamplesPage.js L942-948). Scenario: The user is writing in Eb minor at 145 BPM. The plug-in never shows 145 or Eb minor outside SAMPLES. Every preset preview plays a C-minor phrase. The crowd strum plays C minor pentatonic over an Eb minor song, and wrong notes are never corrected. Fix: Ship doc 20 Stages 0-1 first. Stage 0: a Performer.js MIDI script with KEY, SCALE and SNAP appended at the END of the host parameter list. Stage 1: the header KEY and TEMPO LCD with an APPLY that writes the one global key, which then drives LOOPS shift, the SAMPLES fit, keyboard tint (Engine.setKeyColour), preview transposition and the strum scale. Then add each preset's documented key to FactoryBank. Build PLAYED Key Sense (Stage 2) after that.
- **[major/high] Vocal hook module (doc 22) has zero code, and its Stage 0 disk blocker no longer applies** (Vocal hook (doc 22)). Evidence: Verified.
- No script, page or MIDI processor in the repo mentions hooks or lyrics (grep of scripts/ and hise_project/).
- Doc 22 Stage 0 (L453-460, L566) was blocked on disk space: 7.6 GiB free, measured 2026-11-11.
- `df -h /` today (2026-11-13) shows 100 GiB available on the internal disk.
- Stage 1 needs about 13-15 GB (doc 22 L87). Scenario: The user asked to type lyrics and get a sung hook in at least 10 preset voices. Nothing in the plug-in, or next to it, does this yet. Fix: Bring the doc 22 Stage 1 download list (L566-575) to the user for explicit approval. Nothing downloads without it. Build the local companion renderer first. In the plug-in, a HOOK page then only needs to load the rendered stems into the existing Sample Play sampler with SongKey and host-BPM matching.
Keep the consent rule: own voice or signed singers only, no artist cloning. The Tiger DiffSinger bank stays private test only (CC BY-NC-ND), never a preset.
- **[major/high] No mono, legato or glide (doc 16 Stage 4) and no arp (Stage 5), so Moog-style lines and 808 slides cannot be played live** (Synth engine depth (doc 16)). Evidence: Verified.
- There is no Voicing.js among the ScriptProcessors.
- scripts/deep_params.json has 30 controls and none of them is GLIDE or MODE.
- Voice A is forced poly with SamplerRepeatMode 3 (Interface.js L407).
- Doc 16 Stage 4 (L471-487) designed POLY/MONO/LEGATO plus GLIDE using Message.ignoreEvent and Synth.addPitchFade.
- Stage 5 (L489-505) planned the 4.1.0 hardcoded Arpeggiator.
- The bank has glide-themed sounds that need this to play as named: long_glide_808, molasses_glide_sub, ladder_lead, fat_mono_line. Scenario: The user plays an overlapping two-note line on Long Glide 808 or Ladder Lead. Both notes sound polyphonically with a hard retrigger, with no slide between them. Fix: Build Voicing.js as in doc 16 Stage 4: a non-deferred script after KeyShift. MODE is POLY, MONO or LEGATO. GLIDE is in ms and can be legato-only or always, using addPitchFade on the held event. Apply the same to the Sub. Store MODE and GLIDE in the preset 'd' map, and set legato plus glide on 808 and lead presets. Append the host parameters at the end of the list (doc 16 indices 34-35).
- **[major/high] The movement engine exists but no factory sound uses it, so the bank feels static** (Sound design vs the MOD and SHAPE pages (doc 16)). Evidence: Verified, counted in FactoryBank.js.
- 0 of 197 presets carry 'mod' rows.
- Only 2 presets use the filter envelope (FEnvAmtKnob).
- 33 presets set any SHAPE value, and 31 of those set only DRIFT.
- applyPresetMod (Interface.js L3651-3672) resets every LFO and envelope source and calls Matrix.clearAllConnections on every load.
- The 11-source, 6-target matrix and the per-voice filter therefore stay silent unless the user wires them by hand. Scenario: The user loads a 'ghost' or 'ethereal' pad and holds a chord. Nothing breathes or evolves except the master Movement LFO, which is tied to the MOTION macro. Every preset has the same static core. Fix: Do a sound-design pass that writes 'mod' rows and 'd' values; the check_mod build gate already validates them. Ideas: slow LFO 4 to Cutoff on pads, Random to A Pitch at a few cents for tape-like instability (this needs the fine-pitch fix in another finding), Env 3 to A Level for swells, filter-envelope plucks. Pick 20-30 'mystic' presets first and A/B them blind against the current versions.
- **[major/high] You cannot save your own sound, and any preset click wipes SHAPE edits and MOD routings with no undo** (Preset workflow). Evidence: Verified.
- loadPreset (Interface.js L3693-3719) runs setDeepFromPreset and applyPresetMod, which calls Matrix.clearAllConnections (L3660). The comment at L2581-2582 admits that reloading 'wiped them with no undo'.
- Header prev/next (L1043-1044) and every browser row click call loadPreset.
- UserPresetWidgets.js is never included (includes are at L23-27 and L3747).
- AppData 'User Presets/Factory' holds 5 legacy v0.1 category folders that no page can browse.
- The only persistence is the DAW session (PresetKey, ModState). Scenario: The user builds a haunted pad (filter envelope, LFO to cutoff, drift), then presses the header arrow to compare another sound. Their pad is gone and cannot be recalled outside the saved Live set. Fix: Add MY SOUNDS. A SAVE button writes {base preset id, 8 macros, A/R, every DV and MV value, Matrix.toBase64()} to AppData user_sounds.json. The browser gets a USER pill that loads entries through the same apply functions. Keep a one-step UNDO snapshot before every loadPreset or VARIATION. Delete or wire the dead UserPresetWidgets.js.
- **[major/high] In LOOPS, the macro, ATTACK and RELEASE knobs show values that are not what you hear, and moving them changes the instrument sound** (LOOPS and PLAY flow). Evidence: Verified.
- setPage shows MacroCard, all 8 macros and ATTACK/RELEASE whenever the mode is not SAMPLES, which includes LOOPS (Interface.js L2894-2904).
- applyLoopFx (L3785-3797) quietly sets the modules to a clean state (TONE 20 kHz, SPACE 0.08, no ECHO, GRIT or PUMP), but the knobs keep showing the instrument preset's values.
- onMacro (L2495-2500) has no mode check, so turning a knob in LOOPS rewrites the module and the host parameter.
- RESET in LOOPS reapplies the clean FX without moving the knobs (L2587-2591).
- ATTACK and RELEASE only drive Amp Env and Sub Env (L479-489), and both synths are bypassed in LOOPS.
- The MACROS strip still prints the instrument preset's ECHO/PUMP/VELOCITY/Q line (L2572). Scenario: In LOOPS the SPACE knob reads 60%, but the loops play at 8% wet. The user nudges it and the reverb jumps to about 33%. ATTACK does nothing at all. Back in PLAY, the instrument preset has silently changed. Fix: In LOOPS, show a dedicated LOOP FX card bound to its own (non-host) state, or hide the macros and ATTACK/RELEASE and show only the controls that work there. At minimum, set the knobs to match applyLoopFx while in LOOPS and restore them on leaving. Drive Loop Env attack and release from that card.
- **[major/high] The texture chain is generic: a Freeverb-based reverb, a fixed EQ, one sample layer and none of the doc 13 Submerge, Tape or Convolution stages** (Engine and FX vs the 'mystic world' request (doc 13)). Evidence: Verified.
- The root FX in XmlPresetBackups/ResearchFacility.xml (L31-82) are: Saturator, ladder Master Filter (Mode 8), Air EQ, Chorus, Delay and SimpleReverb.
- The HISE 4.1.0 SimpleReverb.h L47 describes itself as 'a algorithmic reverb based on Freeverb' (local copy of the 4.1.0 tag in scratch).
- The instrument is one StreamingSampler (L189) plus a SineSynth sub (L306). There is no second or atmosphere layer, no Convolution, no ShapeFX, no ReleaseTrigger and no sample-start scatter.
- Doc 13 actions L33 and L74-77 (Submerge, Tape stage) and NM4 convolution were never built.
- Reverb FreezeMode exists in the XML (L78-79) but has no control. The 4.1.0 header labels it 'unused' (SimpleReverb.h L57), yet it is passed on to the JUCE Reverb parameters (L109). That it gives a working infinite wash is inferred from https://docs.juce.com/master/structReverb_1_1Parameters.html. Scenario: The user wants a sound to feel like a place: fog, a stone room, a worn tape memory. Every preset ends in the same Freeverb tail and the same chorus, and there is no way to lay a room tone or texture bed under a melody. Fix: In this order:
(1) Add a 'Layer B / Atmos' StreamingSampler with level, filter and a texture picker, filled with the user's own field recordings and room tones.
(2) Add a Convolution module on a parallel send, loaded with IRs the user records (clap or sweep in a stairwell, church or car) so the licence is clean.
(3) Add ShapeFX Reduce plus the filter and Air EQ on one SUBMERGE macro (doc 13 L74).
(4) Add a tape-wow LFO to A Pitch at cents depth.
(5) Add a FREEZE button on Master Reverb FreezeMode (test it first).
Privately sampled Keyscape or Omnisphere sources are personal-only and never go in the public repo.
- **[major/medium] The build in Live is probably 4 commits behind HEAD, so SAMPLES and the f41a786 fixes may be missing** (Install state). Evidence: Inferred.

Then run the doc 21b probes P1-P4 that are still owed: drag into an audio track, 140 BPM sync, keys without a click, and an unarmed track.
- **[minor/high] The SAMPLES 'RAW' audition is not raw, and LOOPS 'clean FX' is not clean: a hidden Air EQ adds +2.5 dB at 120 Hz and +2 dB at 9 kHz** (SAMPLES (doc 21), LOOPS). Evidence: Verified.
- XML L66: Air EQ Bypassed=0. Band0-4 are gain 2.5, 120 Hz, Q 0.7, enabled, type 2. Band5-9 are gain 2.0, 9000 Hz, Q 0.7, enabled, type 3.
- The 4.1.0 CurveEq.h L68-87 band order is Gain, Freq, Q, Enabled, Type, where 2 = LowShelf and 3 = HighShelf.
- smpRawFx (SamplesPage.js L1505-1513) bypasses Filter, Drive, Chorus, Delay and Reverb, but not Air EQ.
- applyLoopFx (Interface.js L3785-3797) also leaves it on.
- Interface.js never resolves 'Air EQ', so no control reaches it. Scenario: The user picks a bass loop by ear in SAMPLES, then drags the original file into Live. The file sounds thinner and darker than what they auditioned, so the choice was made on a coloured signal. Fix: Add FX_Air = Synth.getEffect("Air EQ"). Bypass it in smpRawFx and applyLoopFx. Expose AIR (gain and shelf frequencies) on an FX page or fold it into the TONE macro, so it is no longer a hidden always-on colour.
- **[minor/high] Dead and placeholder controls: REPEAT and +STACK, PREVIEW and strum in LOOPS, DRAWN and STEPS LFOs with no editor, and an unlabeled TRIP toggle** (UI controls (docs 16, 18b, 21)). Evidence: Verified unless marked.
- SAMPLES draws REPEAT and +STACK as greyed pills that do nothing (SamplesPage.js L1341-1351).
- In LOOPS the PREVIEW pill is still drawn (Interface.js L2113-2122), but its click only works when the mode is 0 (L2188). The cover text 'PLAY A KEY / CLICK THE CROWD TO STRUM / PRESS PREVIEW' also shows in LOOPS (L2143), where strum is disabled too (L2190-2196).
- MOD offers the DRAWN and STEPS LFO waves (L3277), but there is no table or slider-pack editor. The XML has CustomWaveform="" and the default StepData "4......." (L99-136). That this gives little or no audible stepping is inferred.
- STAGE TRIP (HOUSE/CHILL/SHOW/RAVE) only cycles when you click the 'STAGE / SHOW' text (L2166-2170), and it resets every session because it is not a saved control. Scenario: The user clicks +STACK, or PREVIEW in LOOPS, or picks STEPS on LFO 3, and nothing audible happens. They conclude the plug-in is broken. Fix: Hide controls that are not built yet, or give them a 'coming' tooltip. In LOOPS, swap PREVIEW for a kit audition and replace the cover text with the loop-key hint. Add a ScriptSliderPack for LFO 3 and a ScriptTable for LFO 4 (doc 16 Stage 3), saved in the session. Turn TRIP into a visible stepper backed by a hidden saved label.
- **[minor/high] MOD pitch depth snaps to whole semitones, so LFO-to-pitch cannot do tape wow, chorus drift or subtle vibrato** (MOD page (doc 16 Stage 2)). Evidence: Verified. setConnDepth rounds A Pitch intensity: Math.round(frac * 24.0 - 12.0) (Interface.js L3583). New pitch routes start at 1.0 semitone (L3100, L3251). The row shows whole semitones (L3539). Scenario: The user routes LFO 4 to PITCH for a warped-tape feel. The only choices are 0 or at least 1 semitone of wobble, which is far too much for wow. Fix: Use 0.01-semitone resolution, with Shift-drag for fine control. Show cents below 1 semitone and default pitch routes to 0.15 st. Optionally add a separate 'Wow' preset row: LFO 4 drawn shape to A Pitch at about 0.1 st.
- **[minor/high] Only 10 host parameters: none of the SHAPE or MOD controls can be automated in Live** (Host automation (doc 16 'Host parameters')). Evidence: Verified.
- The host parameters are the 8 macros (Interface.js L2298-2299) plus Attack and Release (L2506, L2510).
- All 30 SHAPE controls are created with isPluginParameter false (L2852), and so are the 24 MOD source controls (L3367).
- Doc 16 indices 22-37 (Cutoff, Resonance, Filter Env, Vibrato, LFO rates, Glide, Voice Mode, Arp) were gated on a doc 14 lock commit that never landed (doc 16b 'Not done yet'). Scenario: The user wants the voice-filter cutoff to open slowly over a 16-bar build, or the LFO rate to speed up into a drop. Neither shows up in Live's automation lanes. Fix: Append the doc 16 parameter list at the END of the current list, so saved Live Sets keep their indices: Cutoff, Reso, Filter Env amount and decay, Vibrato depth and rate, Amp Decay and Sustain, LFO 1-4 rates, then Glide and Mode when Stage 4 lands. Add a selfTest that checks the parameter count and order.
- **[minor/high] No FX page: echo time, PUMP rate, reverb size and EQ are fixed per preset or buried in macro fan-outs** (Porcelain Lab FX page (doc 17 page 5)). Evidence: Verified.

- PUMP is locked to a quarter note (L420-425), and the MACROS strip hard-codes 'PUMP 1/4' (L2572).

Fix: Build the doc 17 FX page at the current 1024x700 size first. Give it DELAY TIME L/R stepped by tempo name, PUMP RATE (1/2, 1/4, 1/8), REVERB SIZE, DAMP and FREEZE, and AIR EQ gains. These are module attributes, so they save with the preset or session and need no new DSP.
- **[minor/high] Porcelain Lab (doc 17) is only half ported: the layout is still the v0.2 1024x700 shell** (Look and feel (doc 17, 18b)). Evidence: Verified.

Replace the Pillow plate with the doc 18b Stage 3 Blender plates (each render needs the user's OK first). Remove or wire the unused assets and scripts.
- **[minor/high] The stage visual is only a festival concert: no 'world' or mystic scenes, and in LOOPS it shows the instrument preset's look instead of the kit's** (Stage (doc 18b)). Evidence: Verified.
