- There are 13 scene names (L1472-1477) and 7 rigs (L1480-1488). None is mystic, ritual or forest, even though kits are tagged mythical (6), eerie (3), ritual (3) and haunted (2) in LoopBank.js.
- loadKit (L3768-3781) never calls buildVD, so in LOOPS the look comes from FACTORY_BANK[S.cur].
- Doc 18b Stage 2 (BUILD detector, VISUALS OFF, haze, lasers) and Stage 3 (Blender plates) are not built (doc 18b L628-660). Scenario: The user loads the 'Seance Tape' or 'Omen Bells' kit and gets the bright DEEP or THUMP club rig of whatever instrument was loaded before. The visual tells the opposite story from the sound. Fix: Call buildVD from the kit's tags in loadKit. Add world scenes that reuse the existing layer system: fog shrine, candle vigil, underwater temple, desert night. Map them to tags such as mythical, ritual, haunted and underwater. Add the VISUALS OFF toggle (also saves CPU). Keep flashOK (WCAG 2.3.1) for every scene.
- **[minor/medium] Mac keys always play velocity 127, and an octave shift carries into LOOPS where most Mac keys then go silent** (Playing with the Mac keyboard). Evidence: Verified.
- KeyShift.js identifies Mac keys by velocity == 127 on notes 60-76 (L55-58), so every Mac-key note hits the top velocity layer at full VEL>AMP.
- setMode (Interface.js L3858-3887) never resets S.shift or Globals.keyShift.
- The loop zone is fixed at 60-75 (LoopBank.js, L638). With S.shift = +1, the Mac keys send 72-88 and only 72-75 trigger loops.
- The LOOPS help text still says 'Mac keys A to P' (L1139). Scenario: The user plays a melody on the laptop keys and every note is equally loud. Or they press ] in PLAY, switch to LOOPS, and A, W, S, E play AIR loops while the rest of the row is silent. Fix: Detect Mac keys some other way (for example a dedicated channel from the floating keyboard path) so velocity is free. Then add a Mac velocity row (keys 1-5 for about 40/64/90/110/127) with a header readout. On entering LOOPS, temporarily zero the shift, or show the shifted letters in the help text.
- **[major/high] Sampling depth is well below the doc 13 bar: no round robins anywhere, 1-2 velocity layers, no release samples** (Authenticity of the core sounds (doc 13)). Evidence: Verified with /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-noxvitae/0e6ab598-c428-4549-a613-b529408ad567/scratchpad/wf_mystic/maps_audit.py over all 197 preset maps.
- None of the 197 maps has more than one RRGroup.
- Median velocity layers: keys 2, chords 2, pads 1, leads 1, plucks 1.
- Zone roots sit every 6 semitones in keys, pads, leads, bells, bass and plucks.
- There is no ReleaseTrigger sampler and no sample-start Random in the XML.
- Doc 13 L30 sets the bar at 4-6 velocity layers, 2-4 round robins, velocity crossfades and real release samples. L629 adds sample-start scatter as a free pseudo round robin. Scenario: Repeated notes on Night Pluck Arp, the nylon ostinatos or the 808 rolls machine-gun: every hit is byte-identical. Soft and hard playing only change the volume, never the timbre. Fix: In this order:
(1) Now: add a Random modulator to Voice A's sample-start (doc 13 L629), plus a per-RR seeded variation in build_signature_bank.py that renders 2-3 RRs with different noise, drift and pick seeds (doc 14 rank 6).
(2) Render 4 velocity layers for keys and plucks, with UpperVelocityXFade.
(3) Later: a release layer through the ReleaseTrigger processor from the user's own recordings.
- **[major/high] Doc 14's do-next melody helpers are missing: chord colours, roll and drift feel, MIDI out, and SLOWED with fine TUNE** (Melody workflow (doc 14 ranks 3, 5, 7, 8)). Evidence: Verified.
- No chord-mode or progression code exists in Interface.js or the MIDI scripts.
- SEMI is whole semitones only: ASemiKnob step 1, range -12..12 (scripts/deep_params.json). There is no ±50-cent TUNE and no SLOWED ratio, which doc 14 rank 8 calls high value because slowed uploads sit at 0.72x-0.89x.
- There is no plug-in MIDI out, so preview, strum and any future chord notes cannot be recorded in Live (doc 14 rank 7).
- There is no strum or roll humanize (doc 14 rank 5). Scenario: The user wants a slowed+reverb Rhodes. They can't detune by 30 cents to sit with a slowed sample, can't play a maj9 with one finger, and can't capture the plug-in's strum into a clip. Fix: Fold these into the Performer.js from the key-lock finding: CHORD (palette from doc 14 rank 3), ROLL ms, TIMING drift, then MIDI out on VST3. Add a TUNE ±50 c control (A Pitch UIMod accepts fractional semitones) and a SLOWED knob that couples tune, envelope time scale and the TONE darkening.
- **[minor/high] LOOPS discovery and feedback are thin: no search, filter, favourites or audition for 64 kits, and no bar position or stretch readout** (LOOPS browser (doc 15, doc 17 page 4)). Evidence: Verified.
- The kit list shows 7 rows per page with arrow paging (Interface.js L1058-1114), so 64 kits take 10 pages.
- The search field is hidden in LOOPS (L3879), and there are no tag chips or favourites, although the kits carry about 250 distinct tags.
- loadKit calls LoopSampler.loadSampleMap (L3776-3777), which kills running loops, so switching kits is never seamless.
- No phase wheel or bar counter exists (doc 17 LOOPS hero).
- The header shows only the kit BPM and key, never the host BPM or the half/double feel that applyFeel picks (L605-630, L955). Scenario: The user hunts for an eerie kit at 70 BPM and has to page through 10 screens reading tag strings. Switching kits mid-jam cuts everything off. At 145 BPM they can't tell the loops are playing in half-time feel. Fix: Add a kit search plus tag chips (mythical, eerie, ritual, latin, 808, piano and so on) and kit hearts. Add a KIT PREVIEW that plays take 1 of KEYS for one loop. Queue kit switches to the next loop boundary through the router. Add a small phase ring per lane and a header readout such as 'HOST 145 / KIT 72 x2 feel'.
- **[idea/high] Generic copy and labels undercut the 'world' feeling** (Text and voice of the UI). Evidence: Verified.
- All 50 ARCHIVE presets have placeholder descriptions such as 'Original Research Facility lab specimen (basse).', including the typo 'basse' (FactoryBank.js). They appear on the stage lower third (Interface.js L2107).
- The macro card always reads 'Hover a knob for its value. Every macro is automatable from the host.' (L2578) and 'PUMP 1/4' (L2572).
- All sounds share the same eight macro labels and tips (L155-163).
- The LOOPS lower third reads '16 original loops at X BPM in key' (L2107).
- Doc 12 has per-preset play tips (roll timings, register) that are never shown (doc 14 rank 16). Scenario: The user reads spec-sheet sentences instead of a one-line scene or play tip, so the sounds feel like lab items rather than places. Fix: Write a one-line scene and play tip for each preset from doc 12 (for example 'roll the bass first, top 30-60 ms later'). Give each sound its own subtitles for 2-3 macros (doc 14 rank 16). Rewrite the archive descriptions and fix 'basse'. Show the kit's own tags and progression in the LOOPS lower third.
- **[idea/medium] Loop content: 16-bit renders, the same instruments reused across kits, no percussion or texture lane, and identical repeated hits** (Loop content (doc 15)). Evidence: Verified unless marked.
- All 1,024 LL_ files are 48 kHz/16-bit, while every SIG_ file is 24-bit. build_loop_lines.py L228 says 16-bit was chosen so 60+ kits would fit on disk, and the disk now has 100 GiB free.
- wood_bass_pluck is the BASS lane in 14 of 64 kits.
- Lanes are KEYS, BASS, PLUCK and AIR only (LoopBank.js).
- 59 of 64 kits are 2 bars long.
- render_loop caches each rendered note by (note, length) with a fixed seed (build_loop_lines.py L160-168), so repeated notes in a loop are the same waveform. That this is audible as mechanical on arps is inferred. Scenario: After a few kits the bass lanes start to sound alike. The 2-bar cycles feel short for writing toplines, and the kits carry harmony but no ritual or room texture of their own. Fix: Render new kits at 24-bit. Vary the RNG seed per hit, not per note. Add an optional 5th TEXTURE lane (own field recordings, tape hiss, room tone) or a 4-bar 'B section' take. Rotate bass presets so no single lane preset appears in more than about 5 kits.
- **[idea/high] VARIATION has no undo, and the v0.1 A/B macro compare is gone** (PLAY macros). Evidence: Verified.
- variation() randomizes all 8 macros, including TONE by up to about ±0.6 octave (Interface.js L2604-2616), and keeps no snapshot.
- CHANGELOG.md 0.1.0 lists an 'A/B macro compare in the Quick Tweak view', but Interface.js has no A/B or compare code today. Scenario: The user hits VARIATION on a pad they liked, gets something worse, and cannot get back except with RESET, which also throws away their own macro moves. Fix: Snapshot the macros, A/R and DV before each VARIATION. Add A and B slots with a toggle on the MACROS tape. Make VARIATION key-aware and bounded per macro, for example keeping TONE within ±1/3 octave on dark presets.

### Fact-check (25 items checked by an adversarial verifier)

- **CONFIRMED**: Loop lanes start unquantized (no SNAP). Verified. SampleRouter.js L51 returns early unless the note is on channel 15, so only SAMPLES auditions get delayEvent (L53-64). KeyShift.js L71-90 latches loop-zone notes with makeArtificial and never delays them. There is no LoopRouter.js in ScriptProcessors, although doc 15 L92-93 and L149-152 design one. Interface.js has no SNAP control.
- **CONFIRMED**: LOOPS has no song key (22 keys). Verified. Normalised to tonic plus major/minor, the 64 kits sit in 22 keys: A minor 6, F# minor 5, Db major 5, E/F/B/D/C minor 4 each. Counting the modal labels as written, there are 56 distinct key strings. No script calls setCoarseDetune, addPitchFade or setTransposeAmount. In LOOPS the header shows only kit.bpm and kit.key (Interface.js L955).
- **CONFIRMED**: No melody over loops in LOOPS; lane instruments unnamed. Verified. setMode bypasses Voice A and Sub whenever m != 0 (Interface.js L3870-3871). All 64 LL_ maps have zones only on notes 60-75 (LoKey/HiKey grep). All 124 distinct lane preset ids exist in FactoryBank. The UI draws only KEYS/BASS/PLUCK/AIR (L1126-1136), and the lower third reads 'KEYS C3 / BASS E3 / PLUCK G#3 / AIR C4' (L2110). Seance Tape's PLUCK lane is toy_box_piano.
- **CONFIRMED**: Key and BPM scan missing; previews and strum hard-wired to C minor. Verified. No Performer.js exists. FactoryBank entries have no key field. The strum uses STRUM_SCALE = [0,3,5,7,10] from note 36 (L1735, L2154), which is C minor pentatonic. PREVIEW falls back to 92 BPM (L748). Only SAMPLES shows host BPM and a key picker (SamplesPage.js L942-948).
- **CONFIRMED**: Vocal hook (doc 22) has zero code; disk blocker gone. Verified. No file in scripts/, hise_project/ or src/ mentions lyrics, DiffSinger or a hook page. Doc 22 L85-88 recorded 7.6 GiB free. Today 'df -h' shows 99 GiB available on / (the finding said 100).
- **CONFIRMED**: No mono, legato, glide or arp (doc 16 Stages 4-5). Verified. There is no Voicing.js. deep_params.json has 30 params, none of them glide or mode. Interface.js L407 sets only SamplerRepeatMode 3 (KillSecondOldestNote). Doc 16 L471-505 designs Stages 4 and 5. long_glide_808, molasses_glide_sub, ladder_lead and fat_mono_line all exist in FactoryBank.
- **CONFIRMED**: Movement engine unused by factory sounds. Verified by parsing FactoryBank.js: 0 of 197 presets have 'mod' rows, and only 2 (rain_neon_brass, wet_wire_squelch) set FEnvAmtKnob. applyPresetMod resets the sources and calls Matrix.clearAllConnections (L3651-3672). The matrix has 11 sources and 6 targets (L3164, L3178).
- **CONFIRMED**: Cannot save own sound; preset load wipes SHAPE and MOD. Verified. loadPreset (L3693-3719) calls setDeepFromPreset and applyPresetMod, which calls Matrix.clearAllConnections. UserPresetWidgets.js and VuMeter.js are never included (includes at L23-27 and L3747). AppData 'User Presets/Factory' holds Basses, Leads, Pads, Plucks and Textures that no page browses. The L2581-2582 comment is about RESET's design, but it does confirm that a reload wipes edits.
- **CONFIRMED**: LOOPS macro, ATTACK and RELEASE knobs show false values. Verified. setPage gives play = !shape && !mod && S.mode != 2, so LOOPS shows the macros and A/R (L2891-2904). onMacro has no mode gate (L2495-2500). applyLoopFx sets clean module values without moving the knobs (L3785-3797). SPACE at 60% gives 0.6 x 0.55 = 0.33 wet (L466). A/R drive only Amp Env and Sub Env (L479-489), and Loop Lines has its own Loop Env (XML L388).
- **CONFIRMED**: Texture chain generic (Freeverb, no convolution or atmos layer). Verified. The root FX in the XML are Drive, Master Filter, Air EQ, Chorus, Space Delay and Master Reverb (SimpleReverb). The HISE 4.1.0 SimpleReverb.h L40 even describes it as the JUCE Freeverb that 'does not sound very good', and L47 calls it 'based on Freeverb'. There is one StreamingSampler plus the SineSynth sub, no Convolution, ShapeFX or ReleaseTrigger. FreezeMode is labelled unused (L57) but is passed to the JUCE parameters (L109); that it works is inferred.
- **CONFIRMED**: Build in Live is behind HEAD. Verified. installed_build.txt names run 43268058882, commit 60875b63. Both AU and VST3 binaries are dated Nov 12 13:58 local time (AST, UTC-4); the stamp's '12:59' was written at UTC-5, so it is the same moment, 17:59 UTC. Commit f41a786 ('fixes from the 60875b6 Live test') was made 8 minutes later, at 18:07 UTC. Nothing has been installed since.
- **CONFIRMED**: Hidden Air EQ colours SAMPLES audition and LOOPS clean FX. Verified. XML L66: Band0-4 = 2.5 dB, 120 Hz, Q 0.7, enabled, type 2; Band5-9 = 2.0 dB, 9000 Hz, Q 0.7, enabled, type 3. HISE 4.1.0 CurveEq.h orders each band Gain, Freq, Q, Enabled, Type, with LowShelf = 2 and HighShelf = 3, and restoreFromValueTree reads Band0..N in that order. smpRawFx (SamplesPage.js L1505-1513) and applyLoopFx skip it, and no script calls getEffect('Air EQ').
- **CONFIRMED**: Dead or placeholder controls. Verified. REPEAT and +STACK are drawn greyed with no action (SamplesPage.js L1341-1351). PREVIEW is drawn in LOOPS but runs only when S.mode == 0 (L2188), and strum is gated the same way (L2190-2198), while the cover text still offers both (L2143). LFO_WAVES offers DRAWN and STEPS (L3277) with no editor. TRIP cycles only on the 'STAGE / SHOW' label (L2079, L2166-2170), and STG.tripI is not a saved control.
- **CONFIRMED**: MOD pitch depth snaps to whole semitones. Verified. setConnDepth computes Math.round(frac * 24 - 12) for 'A Pitch' (L3583). New pitch routes start at 1.0 (L3100, L3251). The row prints one decimal plus 'st' (L3539), but the value is always an integer. ASemiKnob, the target's base control, also has step 1 over -12..12.
- **CONFIRMED**: Only 10 host parameters. Verified. isPluginParameter is true only for the 8 macros (L2298) plus Attack and Release (L2506, L2510). The SHAPE controls (L2852) and the MOD controls (L3367) set it to false.
- **CONFIRMED**: No FX page. Verified. Delay time comes from the preset's dly field only (applyVoicing). PUMP is fixed at tempoIndex('1/4') (L420-425), and 'PUMP 1/4' is hard-coded in the info strip (L2572). Reverb room, damping and width move only through the TONE, WIDTH and SPACE macros (L431-468).
- **CONFIRMED**: Porcelain Lab only half ported. Verified. makeFrontInterface(1024, 700) at L20, while the tokens assume W 1200, H 800 and statusH 24 (L125-126). T.MONO falls back to 'Oxygen' (L122). The header reads 'V0.2' (L913). build_stage_assets.py L3 says 'Pillow only, no Blender'. Nothing references sm_backdrop.png, sm_backdrop_dim.png, VuMeter.js or UserPresetWidgets.js.
- **CONFIRMED**: Stage visual has no world or mystic scenes; LOOPS keeps the instrument look. Verified. L1770: 'festival main stage seen from the crowd'. SCENE_NAME has 13 distinct names and LOOKS has 7 rigs. loadKit (L3768-3781) never calls buildVD.
- **CONFIRMED**: Mac keys fixed at velocity 127; octave shift carries into LOOPS. Verified. KeyShift.js L57 treats channel 16, notes 60-76 at velocity 127 as Mac keys. Every SIG map splits velocity at 85, so these always hit the top layer. setMode never resets S.shift or Globals.keyShift. Loop zones are 60-75, so with shift +1 only 72-75 (A, W, S, E) play, and those are the AIR lane.
- **CONFIRMED**: Sampling depth: no round robins, 1-2 velocity layers, no release samples. Verified. Across all 264 maps, every one of the 3,829 zones is RRGroup 1. All 2,752 SIG zones have UpperVelocityXFade 0. Velocity layers top out at 2 in every category; the medians are 2 for keys and chords and 1 for pads, leads and plucks. The XML has no ReleaseTrigger.
- **CONFIRMED**: Doc 14 melody helpers missing (chords, roll, MIDI out, SLOWED/TUNE). Verified. ASemiKnob has step 1 over -12..12 (deep_params.json). There is no chord, roll or MIDI-out code. Doc 14 L17 ranks 'SLOWED ratio + TUNE +/-50 cents' do-next, with slowed uploads at 0.72x to 0.89x, and L16 ranks MIDI out, which L69 limits to VST3 because AU cannot send MIDI out in Live.
- **CONFIRMED**: LOOPS discovery and feedback thin. Verified. kitVis() shows 7 rows, so 64 kits take 10 pages. SearchField.showControl(m == 0) hides search in LOOPS (L3879). Kits carry 257 distinct tags. loadKit calls LoopSampler.loadSampleMap (L3776-3777). The header shows only the kit's BPM and key (L955), and applyFeel picks x2 or /2 silently (L612-614).
- **CONFIRMED**: Generic copy and labels. Verified. All 50 ARCHIVE descriptions read 'Original Research Facility lab specimen (...)', and 10 of them say '(basse)'. The macro card text is fixed (L2572, L2578). The LOOPS lower third reads '16 original loops at X BPM in key' (L2107).
- **CONFIRMED**: Loop content: 16-bit, reused lane presets, identical repeated hits. Verified. All 1,024 LL_ files are 48 kHz, 16-bit, stereo, and all 2,752 SIG_ files are 24-bit (header scan). build_loop_lines.py L228 writes 16-bit 'so 60+ kits fit on disk'. wood_bass_pluck is the BASS lane in 14 of 64 kits. 59 of 64 kits are 8 quarters (2 bars).
- **CONFIRMED**: VARIATION has no undo; A/B compare gone. Verified. variation() changes TONE by 2^((rand - 0.5) x 1.2), which is up to +/-0.6 octave, and the other macros by +/-0.12, with no snapshot (L2604-2616). CHANGELOG.md L14 lists 'A/B macro compare'. No compare or undo code exists.
